### [1] [Tripartite nonlocal magic from reflected and negativity spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13071)
*Sriram Akella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可计算且可实验测量的三体非局域魔性诊断方法，基于边缘、反射和负性谱（符号除外）的平坦性条件，并在非阿贝尔量子双模型和随机张量网络中给出了其出现的具体条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了量化和检测三体纯态中的非局域魔性，需要一种既理论严谨又实验可行的判据。

Method: 利用边缘谱、反射谱和负性谱（忽略符号）的平坦性作为判据，并引入‘反平坦性’进行量化；结合随机测量技术实现实验可测性；在非阿贝尔量子双和随机张量网络中进行模型验证。

Result: 确立了三体纯态局域幺正等价于稳定子态的充要条件；提出了可计算、可测量的非局域魔性诊断工具；揭示了其在非阿贝尔量子双（三划分点位于plaquette上）和随机张量网络（两边界区域的纠缠楔连通时）中的出现机制。

Conclusion: 三体非局域魔性可通过谱平坦性严格刻画，该特性不仅具理论意义，还可通过随机测量实验探测，并在不同量子多体模型中展现出普适的几何或拓扑起源。

Abstract: A tripartite pure state is local-unitary equivalent to a stabilizer state if and only if its marginal, reflected, and negativity spectra (up to signs) are flat. These conditions are quantified using anti-flatness, giving a diagnostic of tripartite nonlocal magic that is computable and experimentally measurable using randomized measurements. In non-abelian quantum doubles, it appears only when the triple points of the tripartition lie on plaquettes. In random tensor networks, it appears exactly when the entanglement wedge of two boundary regions connects.

</details>


### [2] [Four-charge Rotating AdS Black Holes in Gauged STU Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13074)
*Liang Ma,H. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了四维规范STU超引力中具有四个独立电荷的非极端旋转AdS黑洞的精确解，并验证了其热力学性质及BPS极限下的熵与对偶CFT微观结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于规范引入的标量势破坏了未规范理论中解生成方法所依赖的隐藏对称性，构造此类解长期存在挑战。

Method: 通过直接求解规范STU超引力场方程，构造非极端旋转AdS黑洞的精确解，并分析其全局性质与热力学量；随后取BPS极限并比对CFT微观熵。

Result: 得到了四电荷非极端旋转AdS黑洞的精确解，导出了其热力学量，并在BPS极限下验证熵与对偶CFT微观计算结果一致。

Conclusion: 成功克服了规范理论中隐藏对称性破缺带来的困难，为AdS/CFT对应中黑洞微观熵研究提供了新的精确解支持。

Abstract: We present an exact solutions for non-extremal rotating AdS black holes with four independent charges in four-dimensional gauged STU supergravity. The scalar potential introduced by gauging breaks the hidden symmetry underlying solution-generating methods in the ungauged theory, making the construction of such solutions a longstanding challenge. We analyse the global properties of the solution and derive its thermodynamic quantities. We further take the BPS limit and show that the resulting entropy agrees with the known microscopic result obtained from the dual conformal field theory.

</details>


### [3] [More physics at the entangling surface: boundary conditions and entanglement in 2d TQFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13078)
*Jackson R. Fliss,Alan Rios Fukelman,Stathis Vitouladitis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了二维拓扑量子场论（TQFT）中的纠缠性质，指出传统基于路径积分的割截映射通常不保持范数（非等距），提出通过引入边缘算符构造等距割截映射，从而严格定义并计算任意态、任意划分和边界条件下的纠缠；由此发现即使所有边界条件均能隙化，仍可出现非整数的‘基态简并度’；进一步证明可通过单个态的多区间Rényi熵重构任意亏格的配分函数，并利用对称基态的纠缠熵与真空的纠缠不对称性分别探测对称保护相与对称破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清二维TQFT中纠缠测量对希尔伯特空间分解所依赖的割截映射的敏感性，解决传统路径积分割截映射不等距导致纠缠定义不严谨的问题。

Method: 提出并构造等距割截映射——在路径积分基础上引入合适的边缘算符进行‘修饰’；在此框架下统一计算任意态、任意空间划分及边界条件下的纠缠熵与Rényi熵；结合对称性分析与拓扑数据重构技术。

Result: 1) 给出构造任意2d TQFT等距割截映射的普适方案；2) 发现非整数量子化的‘基态简并度’；3) 实现从单态多区间Rényi熵重构任意亏格配分函数；4) 利用纠缠熵区分相同对称性的不同gapped相，用纠缠不对称性检测对称破缺；5) 在有限群规范理论、Haagerup相及adjoint QCD₂红外相中验证该形式体系。

Conclusion: 纠缠在2d TQFT中并非仅由拓扑决定，而强烈依赖于实现希尔伯特空间分解的等距结构；引入边缘算符的等距割截是定义和计算纠缠的必要前提；该框架为利用纠缠探测量子相、对称性与拓扑提供了统一且严格的工具。

Abstract: We present a systematic treatment of entanglement in two-dimensional topological quantum field theories (TQFTs). Our approach clarifies the sensitivity of entanglement measures to the cutting maps that implement Hilbert space factorization. Such maps embed the TQFT Hilbert space into a description with potentially more degrees of freedom, and for entanglement to be well defined they must be isometric, i.e., they must preserve the norms of all states. We show that the naïve cutting map based solely upon path integration subject to boundary conditions is not isometric for generic TQFTs. For any 2d TQFT, we give a prescription to construct isometric cutting maps by dressing path integration with suitable edge operators. This allows us to compute entanglement for any state, any bipartition, and any boundary condition. As a consequence, we find a non-integer "ground-state degeneracy" à la Affleck-Ludwig, despite all boundary conditions being gapped. We further show how to reconstruct the partition function of the TQFT at arbitrary genus from the multi-interval Rényi entropies of a single state. For theories with (possibly non-invertible) symmetries, we show that the entanglement entropy of the symmetric ground state can distinguish gapped phases with the same symmetry, without any further knowledge of their vacua, while the entanglement asymmetry of the vacua entropically detects symmetry breaking. We illustrate our formalism with a range of applications, from finite gauge theories to Haagerup gapped phases and infrared phases of adjoint QCD$_2$.

</details>


### [4] [Type IIB instanton corrections from pure modular unfolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13086)
*Amineh Mohseni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用模对称性，而非超对称微分方程，从低阶傅里叶模式重构IIB型弦理论中$D^6R^4$和$D^8R^4$等高导数相互作用的瞬子修正；通过Rankin-Selberg展开、Hecke关系与Kuznetsov迹公式等工具，成功提取并重建了所有高阶傅里叶模式，并确定了弱耦合下瞬子前因子的$g_s^{-1/2}$与常数项，结果与已知展开一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为绕过依赖超对称微分方程的传统方法，探索非BPS高导数相互作用（如$D^6R^4$、$D^8R^4$）的瞬子结构，仅依靠其模系数函数与傅里叶展开的低阶模式。

Method: 结合Rankin-Selberg展开提取连续谱贡献、Mellin余数解析第一模以获取尖点振幅、Hecke关系延拓高阶尖点模、Kuznetsov迹公式与谱尾估计推导弱耦合瞬子前因子，并分析模模糊性及其稳定性条件。

Result: 成功重构$D^6R^4$全部傅里叶模式，导出瞬子前因子中$g_s^{-1/2}$与常数项，与已知结果一致；对$D^8R^4$，明确了预测高电荷项所需的额外信息。

Conclusion: 模对称性与谱分析足以约束非BPS瞬子结构；低阶傅里叶数据配合模不变性可有效重建高阶行为，但需补充特定谱信息（如尖点谱细节）以实现唯一预测。

Abstract: We study the reconstruction of instanton corrections to type IIB higher-derivative interactions from their low Fourier modes using modular symmetry, without invoking supersymmetry differential equations. This provides a framework for studying non-BPS interactions through their modular coefficient functions. For the $D^6R^4$ coefficient, Rankin-Selberg unfolding determines the continuous spectral overlap from the complete zero mode. Assuming the conjectured simplicity of the even cusp spectrum, we extract the cusp amplitudes from Mellin residues of the complete first mode. We combine both contributions to reconstruct all higher Fourier modes, using Hecke relations for the cusp contribution. Using the Kuznetsov trace formula and bounds on the spectral tail, we derive the $g_s^{-1/2}$ and constant terms in the weak-coupling instanton prefactor at every fixed positive charge. The leading term arises collectively from the infinite cusp sum, and both coefficients agree with the known expansion. With incomplete low-mode data, we construct modular ambiguities and give sufficient conditions for the stability of leading higher-charge coefficients. Applied to the supplied $D^8R^4$ data, these results identify the additional information needed for higher-charge predictions.

</details>


### [5] [Holographic Drag Force and the Entanglement Membrane: Geometric Reconstruction and Information Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13088)
*Dmitry S. Ageev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了强耦合系统中耗散拖曳力与纠缠膜之间的精确几何对应关系，建立了非线性动量损失、量子信息传播和近视界谱性质之间的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究全息对偶中不同边界观测量如何编码并重构引力对偶的几何结构。

Method: 通过分析运动重粒子（夸克）所受的有限速度拖曳力与量子信息传播所依赖的纠缠膜张力之间的关系，结合非线性修正、蝴蝶速度、标量极点跳过及随机动量展宽等工具，建立几何重构方法。

Result: 发现拖曳力与纠缠膜张力存在可逆变换；非线性拖曳修正依次编码蝴蝶速度和膜近视界局部几何；拖曳系数与信息速度共同实现内外部时空几何的部分重建；动量展宽补充径向信息以恢复完整外部度规。

Conclusion: 耗散、纠缠动力学与谱性质为全息几何提供了互补的信息通道，形成理解边界观测量几何编码的统一框架。

Abstract: A central question in holography is how different boundary observables encode and reconstruct the geometry of the gravitational dual. In this work, we uncover an exact geometric correspondence between two seemingly distinct aspects of strongly coupled dynamics: the dissipative drag force experienced by a moving heavy particle(quark) and the entanglement membrane governing the propagation of quantum information. We show that both constructions retain the same underlying structure of the geometry, leading to an invertible transformation between the finite-velocity drag force and the geometric membrane tension. This correspondence reveals that nonlinear momentum loss encodes detailed information about entanglement dynamics: the leading nonlinear correction determines the butterfly velocity, while higher-order terms successively probe the local geometry of the membrane near the horizon. We further connect this hierarchy to scalar pole-skipping, establishing an all-order relation between nonlinear dissipation and near-horizon spectral data. The shared geometric structure also has implications for quantum information recovery, with the quintic drag coefficient determining the leading dependence of information velocity on initial entanglement. Conversely, the complete information-velocity curve provides a reconstruction of the reduced bulk geometry along the physical interior membrane branch, complementing the exterior reconstruction from drag. We show that stochastic momentum broadening supplies the additional radial information needed to reconstruct the complete exterior metric up to an overall scale. Our results thus reveal how dissipation, entanglement dynamics, and spectral properties provide complementary access to holographic geometry, establishing a unified framework for understanding the geometric information encoded in distinct boundary observables.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展已知超对称BPS缺陷（如N=4 SYM中的Gukov-Witten面算符、ABJM中的涡旋圈）到非超对称情形，并建立自发对称破缺CFT中缺陷物理与体真空物理的定量联系。

Method: 基于大荷算符鞍点解的解析延拓构造共形缺陷；结合模空间上全局对称性破缺与共形对称性破缺；在量子与半经典区域间插值，利用破缺相低能有效理论进行计算；采用缩放极限分析缺陷算符谱。

Result: 复现了Gukov-Witten面算符的定位计算结果；首次计算了ABJM理论中一大类1/2-BPS涡旋圈的g-函数；建立了缺陷算符谱与破缺真空中质量粒子谱之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种不依赖超对称性、系统研究共形缺陷及其与体理论破缺相联系的新方法，揭示了缺陷物理可由破缺真空的有效理论精确控制。

Abstract: We construct and study a class of codimension-two conformal defects in CFTs with spontaneously broken conformal symmetry, assuming that a continuous global symmetry is also broken on the moduli space. Our construction can be viewed as an analytic continuation of the saddle point describing large charge operators and generalizes known BPS defects in supersymmetric theories, such as Gukov-Witten surface operators in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM and vortex loops in ABJM theory, but does not rely on supersymmetry. These defects are characterized by continuous, unbounded parameters controlling the expectation values of operators parametrizing the moduli space, and interpolate between a quantum regime and a semiclassical regime in which the local physics approaches that of the symmetry-breaking vacuum. This connection allows us to derive exact results from the effective description of the broken phase. In particular, our results for Gukov-Witten surface operators agree with localization, and we compute the $g$-function for a broad class of $1/2$-BPS vortex loops in ABJM theory. Finally, we relate the spectrum of defect operators, in a suitable scaling limit, to the spectrum of massive particles in the symmetry-breaking vacuum.

</details>

*Chuanxin Cui,Kristan Jensen,Sridip Pal,Moshe Rozali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于具有普通或非可逆对称性的局域量子系统的随机矩阵理论（RMT）推广——范畴化RMT，通过管代数作用下的扭曲希尔伯特空间与对称性扇区建模，并在斐波那契任意子链、S₃链、共形场论及全息CFT中验证其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机矩阵理论仅按单一对称性扇区描述混沌系统能级统计，无法处理具有非可逆对称性或多个扭曲希尔伯特空间共享扇区的复杂情形，亟需一种更普适的统计框架。

Method: 引入管代数刻画对称性缺陷定义的扭曲希尔伯特空间及其不可约表示（即对称性扇区），将对称性导致的精确简并与识别关系分离出来，其余部分则沿用传统RMT，不同块间视为统计独立；进一步推广至CFT中的定自旋原初态、连续对称性下的Sugawara构造与谱流，以及全息CFT中的环面虫洞模型。

Result: 确立了范畴化RMT框架：成功解释斐波那契任意子链和S₃混沌链的能级统计；将结果延拓至1+1维CFT中定自旋原初态，发现模协方差引入额外关联；揭示连续对称性下中性谱可呈现辛统计；在全息CFT中，由SymTFT或陈-西蒙斯理论装饰的环面虫洞实现了预测的ramp及其模补全。

Conclusion: 范畴化RMT是传统RMT的重要推广，统一处理可逆与非可逆对称性，在拓扑序、共形场论与量子引力等多领域提供新的能级统计范式。

Abstract: Random matrix theory (RMT) describes the level statistics of chaotic systems one symmetry sector at a time. We propose its analogue for local quantum systems on a circle with ordinary or non-invertible symmetries. Symmetry defects define twisted Hilbert spaces, and the symmetry sectors are irreducible representations of the tube algebra, which acts within and between these spaces. Under our proposal, the symmetry fixes exact degeneracies and identifications among sectors, and ordinary RMT describes the rest, with unrelated blocks treated as statistically independent. We call this categorical RMT. For a non-invertible symmetry it goes beyond ordinary RMT, since Hilbert spaces with different twists can share a sector, and so part of their spectrum, though no invertible symmetry relates them.
  We confirm this in a chaotic Fibonacci anyon chain, and check the framework in chaotic $S_3$ chains. We extend categorical RMT to primary states at fixed spin in 1+1-dimensional CFTs, where modular covariance requires additional correlations. For continuous symmetries, the Sugawara construction and spectral flow isolate a neutral spectrum, which can have symplectic statistics even in a bosonic theory. In holographic CFTs, torus wormholes decorated by the SymTFT or by Chern--Simons theory realize the predicted ramp and its modular completion.

</details>


### [8] [BPS Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13097)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用全息原理，通过边界CFT中的自举方法约束对偶于经典引力几何（尤其是黑洞与平滑几何）的重BPS态的可观测量，以期从第一性原理识别黑洞微观结构特征。

Method: 采用大N矩阵自举技术，在N=4 SU(N)超杨-米尔斯理论弱耦合、大N极限下，针对标度维数为O(N²)的1/2、1/4、1/8和部分1/16-BPS态，构造并求解矩条件与正定性约束。

Result: 在1/2、1/4、1/8-BPS扇区中，自举给出较大允许区域，与大荷极限下的本征值描述一致；在BMN截断的1/16-BPS扇区中，初步结果同时包含对应于无事件视界几何和黑洞的态，但尚无法在自举中明确分离黑洞子集。

Conclusion: 大N矩阵自举能有效刻画平滑BPS几何的模空间结构，但识别黑洞特有的‘岛屿’仍需更精细的约束或更高阶修正；该方法为从CFT出发探测量子引力几何提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We apply the large $N$ matrix bootstrap technique to constrain expectation values of simple operators in heavy BPS states that are dual to classical geometries. For states dual to horizonless geometries, their explicit moduli give rise to large allowable regions in the bootstrap, while for black holes, a converging bootstrap should find islands. We explore this method in $\mathcal{N}=4$ $SU(N)$ super Yang-Mills theory at large $N$, working at weak coupling and focusing on BPS states with scaling dimensions of order $N^2$. For the $1/2$, $1/4$, and $1/8$-BPS sectors, the bootstrap finds large regions, in agreement with an eigenvalue description of such states in the limit of large charges. We also present preliminary results for a BMN truncation of the $1/16$-BPS sector, which contains states dual to both horizonless geometries and black holes. Isolating the black hole states in the bootstrap remains a challenge.

</details>

In fact, a single past merger with mass ratio as small as $q = 0.005$ reduces central densities by 2--3 orders of magnitude, making gravitational-wave dephasing difficult to detect. We relate these results to the MBH merger history by analysing cosmological simulation data from Horizon-AGN. We find that mergers become increasingly common as the MBH mass increases, with mergers of mass ratio q > 0.03 having occurred in 50% of MBHs of mass $3\times10^7M_\odot$ and 95% of mass $10^8M_\odot$ at z=0. These fractions are reached at even lighter masses ($2\times10^7M_\odot$ and $5\times10^7M_\odot$, respectively) at z=3. We find that accretion-induced adiabatic MBH growth is unable to cause appreciable spike re-growth between such mergers in {\tt Horizon-AGN}. Thus, in combination with previous studies of EMRI-induced depletion, this work constrains the mass range over which massive black holes can host detectable DM spikes to $10^6M_\odot$--$5 \times 10^7M_\odot$ for $z \leq 3$.

Abstract: Galaxy clustering could strongly constrain galaxy formation models but is rarely used, because the two-point correlation function (2PCF) requires a complete galaxy catalogue at every point in parameter space. Semi-analytic models (SAMs) process each halo merger tree independently, so running a SAM on $m$ of $k$ disjoint partitions of the trees, each spanning the full box, cuts the cost by $k/m$. Because a halo's galaxies are kept or discarded together, one-halo pairs are diluted less than two-halo pairs, and the naive estimate overstates small-scale clustering by a factor $k/m$. We introduce the Sparse and Unbiased Clustering Estimator (SaUCE), which removes this bias with weights set by $m$ and $k$ alone. It has no free parameters, gives exactly unbiased pair counts, and applies to any catalogue built from a random subset of whole haloes and to any pair-count statistic. Running a SAM on 7 per cent of the P-Millennium merger trees, 68 per cent of random selections recover the full-box $ξ(r)$ at $z=0$ to within 0.05 dex in each bin from 0.03 to $30\,h^{-1}\,\mathrm{Mpc}$, at a density of $10^{-2}\,h^{3}\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-3}$: a fourteen-fold computational saving. The required fraction rises with redshift and as the number density falls, is the same for two model variants, and transfers between boxes through the galaxy and pair counts. The scatter, derived in closed form, is strongly correlated between bins, so a likelihood must use the full covariance. A recipe for choosing the fraction makes the 2PCF affordable for model calibration and simulation-based inference.

Field-level analyses infer bias coefficients at a finite cutoff $Λ$, typically related to the lattice resolution, while $n$-point function analyses typically adopt renormalization conditions defined in the low-$k$ limit. In this work, we use the renormalization group (RG) to relate field-level measurements at different cutoffs and connect them to $n$-point function measurements. We generate mock galaxy fields with a Lagrangian perturbation theory forward model and a galaxy bias expansion, and then measure the bias parameters across a range of cutoffs. We show that the RG predictions describe the measured running and, in the limit $Λ\to 0$, agree with the (tree-level) $n$-point function measurements of first-order $b_δ$ and second-order $b_{δ^2}, b_{{G}_2}$. We also measure the running on halo catalogs based on full $N$-body simulations and show that the RG prediction describes the measurements well within the predicted systematic errors. Our results establish the RG flow as a natural formulation for relating galaxy bias measurements at different cutoffs and connecting the schemes used in field-level and $n$-point function analyses.

Conclusion: 时空模型比共时模型更能协调‘未探测到信号’与‘存在局部连通文明’之间的矛盾；接触本身可能较常见，但完成双向通信（即收到回复）仍属稀有事件；这为SETI策略与文明演化建模提供了新视角。

Abstract: The number of Galactic civilizations does not determine whether they are in contact. Allowing civilizations to appear at different times separates listening, audibility, mutual-listening, and relay networks, with a distinct reply-closing condition requiring a return signal within the sender's lifetime. For each network we derive the contact counts and estimate the connectivity threshold in a uniform population, then apply the results to a structured Galactic disc, separating the detection range $d_{\rm max}$ from the light horizon $cL$ through $χ=d_{\rm max}/cL$. The contactability is $η=πn d_{\rm max}^{2}$, where $n$ is the active civilization surface density. Averaged over civilization appearances, the lifetime listening count is $2η$ for all $χ$ and, at fixed detection range, for any lifetime distribution. Audibility and mutual-listening counts sum to $4η$, but connectivity depends on threshold behavior rather than counts alone. As $χ\rightarrow0$, the listening threshold decreases from the coeval value $4.51$ to $1.32\pm0.01$. Assuming an ideal complete survey, our own non-detection gives the reference bound $η(R_0)\le2.75$ at the Sun's Galactocentric radius. This benchmark lies above the listening, audibility and relay thresholds throughout the range studied. Unlike the coeval model, the spacetime model can satisfy both this benchmark and a local connectivity criterion, moving the local-contact frontier outward by $3.2$ kpc. Under the same benchmark, civilizations hear at most $5.5$ others on average, while the mean reply-closing count falls below unity for $χ\gtrsim1$. Contact may therefore be common even when completed replies remain rare.

Abstract: The Aster library is an open-source collection of numerical tools designed for large-scale gravitational N-body simulations. Aster provides a flexible environment to its users, enabling it to be used in the analysis of a broad range of astrophysical problems. The library implements efficient force calculation algorithms, including a spatial tree solver optimized with coordinate-dependent identifiers, which has been fine-tuned for both CPU and GPU architectures. Despite its high degree of modularity, many parts of the library have been optimized with SIMD instructions, heavy parallelization, and support on dedicated hardware. Aster also offers a wide variety of numerical symplectic integrators, such as SABA (from first to tenth order), the classic leapfrog algorithm, and the more advanced Wisdom-Holman (WH) integrator, while also providing a vast collection of mathematical tools, e.g., post-newtonian expansions (1PN, 2PN, 2.5PN), data collection and graphing systems, a built-in renderer, and presets for faster instantiation of large amounts of bodies. In this work we will discuss in depth the details of each module as well as the results of several performance- and accuracy-related tests, probing the library's capabilities in different physical applications.

</details>

Abstract: Human exploration of Mars will require enormous quantities of materials for the first crewed missions and substantially more for permanent habitation, making flexible and scalable technologies for in situ resource utilization (ISRU), including biological ISRU, of significant interest. However, no existing mission architecture quantitatively evaluates whether in situ biomanufacturing can realistically deliver materials at the scales required for human settlement. Here, we present Mars Seed, an end-to-end reference architecture for using biomanufacturing to produce bioplastics at scale entirely from in situ resources on Mars. We provide a complete analysis of a space bioprocess in which feedstocks are generated entirely from Martian resources, converted biologically into polyhydroxybutyrate (PHB) bioplastic, and purified to produce material suitable for constructing human habitats. We show that the upfront power and upmass costs of deploying the equipment required for this bioprocess are recovered within two synodic periods relative to Earth-shipped alternatives. Further, because the process relies entirely on feedstocks sourced from Martian materials, production can continue until equipment failure without requiring additional materials from Earth, enabling scalable conversion of local resources into useful materials. Mars Seed demonstrates that biological ISRU can be mass- and power-competitive with Earth-shipped alternatives, establishing biomanufacturing as a practical component of Mars mission architecture rather than an aspirational capability.

Abstract: We introduce the NASA-funded mission STARI (STarlight Acquisition and Reflection toward Interferometry), a two-spacecraft technology demonstrator for future long-baseline interferometers. STARI will perform, to our knowledge, the first in-space transfer of starlight between free-flying spacecraft, a critical step toward long-baseline space interferometry. Two propulsive 8U CubeSats in low Earth orbit will fly in formation with separations up to 200 m: one relays a narrow stellar beam to the other, where an off-axis parabola reimages it into a single-mode fiber. Using differential GNSS, visible LED beacons, and fast-steering mirrors in closed-loop control, we aim to sustain diffraction-limited, high-throughput fiber coupling during formation flight. STARI, targeting launch in 2029, is designed to mature key technologies needed for future mission concepts such as the Large Interferometer for Exoplanets (LIFE), which would seek atmospheric biomarkers on nearby Earth-like exoplanets.

</details>

Abstract: Astronomers often have difficulty determining whether an object, such as a star or galaxy of interest, has been discussed in the literature. We bring these results together into GalaxyWiki, where users can find literature discussing galaxies, read what was said about them, and follow links to the source papers. Abstract: Atmospheric variability provides a unique window into the three-dimensional structure, dynamics, and evolution of brown dwarf and giant exoplanet atmospheres. Recent JWST time-resolved spectroscopic observations have revealed complex wavelength-dependent variability, offering unprecedented insights into cloud structures, thermal perturbations, and atmospheric chemistry. However, existing programs have focused primarily on individual benchmarks and specific scientific questions, resulting in heterogeneous spectral and temporal coverage and leaving important regions of the substellar population underexplored, particularly across a broad range of rotation periods, spectral types, and viewing geometries. A JWST Multi-Cycle Treasury program could systematically investigate substellar weather across a diverse, well-characterized population. By combining broad infrared spectroscopic coverage with observations spanning multiple rotations and strategically selected epochs, this program would constrain the contributions of different physical mechanisms while investigating how atmospheric structures depend on rotation, viewing geometry, and fundamental physical properties. Extended monitoring would also improve rotational-period determinations and reveal how atmospheric features evolve from rotation to rotation and over longer timescales. This coordinated effort would provide a consistently designed and uniformly processed dataset of time-resolved spectra and variability diagnostics, enabling population-level comparisons, testing predictions from three-dimensional circulation models, and establishing a framework for interpreting the atmospheric diversity and evolution of brown dwarfs and directly imaged giant exoplanets.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Gaia Data Release 4 (DR4) provides epoch astrometry from which thousands of giant planets and many substellar and stellar companions can be characterised. We present ExoPlanetLab, an open-source package for Bayesian orbit inference from Gaia epoch astrometry, radial velocities (RVs) and relative astrometry, validated on simulated and real data. The model orbit is compared with the epoch residuals after removing the part absorbed by the single-star fit. A Student-t likelihood describes heavy-tailed data. A robust Keplerian periodogram with bootstrap false-alarm probabilities gives starting values for a joint fit. Five samplers are available: tempered ensemble MCMC, two nested samplers and two reversible-jump samplers. Evidences for a fixed number of companions select that number. Acceleration and scan-angle models test whether an orbit is warranted. The samplers agree within 0.1 sigma on a simulated two-planet system. In 180 injections all fits converge, and all signals with signal-to-noise ratio S >= 10 are detected. The posteriors show no significant bias, but the period coverage is below nominal: 90% of the true periods lie within their 95% credible intervals. None of 30 companion-free data sets gives a false detection. On DR4 pre-release data the orbit of Gaia BH3 is recovered within 1.4 sigma of published solutions. The Student-t model brings the astrometric period and eccentricity of HD 114762 onto its RV orbit. Eight simulated relative positions narrow the period's credible interval to 0.27 of its width, and Octofitter agrees within 0.2 sigma on the same data. The code is validated for single sources with DR4-like epoch astrometry, with or without RVs. Relative astrometry is tested on simulated data only. The main limits are an uncorrelated noise model and the computing cost, which grows linearly with the number of measurements and steeply with the number of companions.

</details>

Abstract: Next-generation radio interferometers, such as SKA, will observe the radio sky with unprecedented sensitivity and resolution enabling weak gravitational lensing studies using radio data, which not only complement optical observations but also probe higher redshifts. We then show that applying this parametric model to COSMOS galaxies with realistic morphologies introduces a bias in the shear estimate over 10σ. Abstract: We tackle the problem of detecting faint objects in multivariate data corrupted by a strong and spatially correlated nuisance component in the low-sample regime. We propose a data-driven approach based on sparse precision matrix modeling, where the precision matrix is approximated through a pivoted sparse Cholesky factorization whose non-zeros coefficients are learned by maximum likelihood. The approach is evaluated on an astrophysical application: the detection of exoplanets by high contrast imaging, where it achieves state-of-the-art performance.

Abstract: We investigate heavy-mass vector dark matter within the framework of the $U(1)_X$ gauge Higgs portal vector model, by jointly integrating experimental constraints from relic density measurements, direct detection searches, and indirect detection observations alongside the perturbative criterion to derive bounds on the model parameter space. Within the weakly interacting massive particle scenario, we assess the relative contributions of $s$-channel and $t/u$-channel processes to thermal dark matter annihilation. For parameter configurations lying in the $s$-channel resonance region, each fixed value of the scalar mixing angle $\sinα$ corresponds to a well-defined upper limit on the heavy dark scalar mass $m_{h_2}$. This upper limit decreases monotonically with increasing $\sinα$, with a critical threshold of $\sinα\lesssim 0.05$ beyond which perturbative constraints entirely exclude the full resonance corridor. By contrast, the non-resonant parameter regime dominated by $t/u$-channel annihilation processes is found to be excluded for $m_V \simeq 1$ TeV and above under the combined joint constraints from relic density requirements and perturbative consistency. Our results indicate that the perturbative consistency of the scalar sector, enforced via the two-loop renormalisation group equations, imposes bounds on the parameter space in the heavy mass regime that are comparable to, and in some benchmark regions more restrictive than, current experimental limits. Nevertheless, even under the application of these stringent perturbativity constraints, the model still admits a viable set of parameter points spanning a broad multi-TeV mass range.

Abstract: Conventional helioscope searches are insensitive to the longitudinal component of the solar dark photon (DP) flux, which dominates the solar emission for DP masses $m\lesssim 1\,$eV. As our main result, we find that BabyIAXO would be competitive with the current world-leading constraints from XENON1T if it can keep sensitivity down to energies of $10\,$eV. Abstract: A light scalar present during inflation generically acquires a misalignment and can later constitute the dark matter. We show that a weak coupling to the primordial plasma can relax both bounds simultaneously. The plasma depletes the scalar abundance through thermal damping, a dissipative effect arising from the plasma's finite equilibration time, thereby relaxing the overclosure bound. If the damping grows with the oscillation amplitude, it can also drive different initial amplitudes onto a common attractor that depends only on the local radiation temperature. This synchronizes the scalar with the adiabatic clock, thus suppressing its isocurvature. The resulting isocurvature bound on the inflationary scale relaxes by exactly the factor by which damping reduces the abundance, in addition to the relaxation from the larger initial misalignment now allowed. This result holds for any damping rate quadratic in the amplitude, regardless of its temperature dependence.

Firstly, the authors incorrectly claim that integrands of differential scattering cross-sections are obtained as single "residues" of vacuum amplitudes, although a sharp distinction of initial and final states requires to consider two iterated discontinuities. Secondly, they wrongly state that non-pinched thresholds always locally match in such a differential representation.

Abstract: Starting from on-shell factorization and the polarization sum of an internal massive vector, we provide an off-shell continuation of the transverse and longitudinal contributions defined at arbitrary virtuality. The construction relies on on-shell constructibility and the three-point off-shell current constraint $p \cdot J = O(m)$. For amplitudes with external longitudinal vectors, we develop a method that isolates the mass-singular contributions associated with the longitudinal polarization, namely terms with power-law singularities in the massless limit. In the examples studied, these terms appear as contact contributions in individual factorization channels, and we identify the conditions under which they cancel. We give an independent understanding, based on massive on-shell amplitudes, of the polarization decomposition and diagram-by-diagram high-energy power counting underlying the Feynman-Diagram gauge and related five-component formulations. We further clarify how higher-point current constraints can emerge from on-shell constructibility and the cancellation of mass singularities. We illustrate the mechanism with explicit four-point examples and discuss the constraints imposed by a smooth massless limit. The work provides a systematic bottom-up method for building massive amplitudes free of mass-singular terms and opens a route toward a broader amplitude-based understanding of field decompositions, connecting massive and massless descriptions and on-shell states with off-shell descriptions.

In this context, X- and $γ$-ray observations of the Virgo Cluster through large field-of-view instruments such as eROSITA and COMPTEL yield novel limits on the amplitude of stochastic HFGW backgrounds in the frequency range $10^{17}-10^{22}$ Hz, improving upon constraints derived from the Galactic magnetic field by one to two orders of magnitude.

We then consider more general BSM scenarios with additional HNL interactions, showing how these can modify the main production and decay mechanisms, opening up new signatures beyond those of the minimal benchmark.

Abstract: We present an initial-state implementation of the Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg (KLN) theorem that is compatible with collinear factorisation. In this formulation, hadronic cross-sections are expressed as convolutions of parton distribution functions with partonic cross-sections obtained by summing over the number of incoming partons. By virtue of the KLN theorem, the partonic cross-sections are infrared finite by construction, without relying on dimensional regularisation as an infrared regulator. The Local Unitarity formalism provides a local realisation of this infrared finiteness, allowing partonic cross-sections to be evaluated directly in four dimensions by Monte Carlo integration. We demonstrate the framework at next-to-leading order and leading power. Using Local Unitarity, we compute the Drell-Yan and $t\bar{t}$ production cross-sections numerically; using Reverse Unitarity, we obtain the deep inelastic scattering and Drell-Yan cross-sections analytically. In all cases, a universal scheme conversion relates our results to those obtained in the $\overline{\text{MS}}$ parton model.

</details>

For both this process and the inverse process $γγ\to l^{+}l^{-}$, these corrections can be important for accurate predictions when the lepton mass cannot be neglected, particularly in the near-threshold region. The amplitude is presented in a tensor basis and can therefore be evaluated for arbitrary polarizations of the external particles. All results are expressed in terms of classical polylogarithms $\mathrm{Li}_n$ for efficient numerical evaluation. Numerical tests indicate that the truncated expansions remain accurate for $s,|t|,|u|\gtrsim40m_l^2$. At leading power in the lepton mass, the two-loop amplitude agrees with the prediction of QED mass factorization.

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限温度下无化学势的一味四费米子模型，结合平均场近似和函数重整化群方法，分析了标量、赝标量及混合相互作用对凝聚态和相变的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解热涨落如何抑制自发对称性破缺和费米子不稳定性，同时保留显式对称性破缺效应。

Method: 结合平均场近似（确定平衡凝聚）与函数重整化群（描述相互作用和费米子质量相的尺度依赖性）。

Result: 发现赝标量凝聚自发破缺P/CP对称性，并通过连续热相变消失；高温下FRG轨迹中无量纲混合耦合趋于零，而有量纲耦合保持有限；温度升高也抑制费米子质量相的尺度演化。

Conclusion: 热涨落在抑制自发对称性破缺和费米子不稳定性方面起不同作用，但不一定消除显式对称性破缺效应。

Abstract: We investigate a one-flavor four-fermion model with scalar, pseudoscalar, and mixed scalar-pseudoscalar interactions at finite temperature and vanishing chemical potential. Our analysis combines the mean-field approximation, which determines the equilibrium condensates, with the functional renormalization group (FRG), which describes the scale dependence of the interactions and the fermion mass phase. Within the mean-field approach, we find that, in the absence of explicit symmetry-breaking sources, the dominant interaction determines the direction of condensation. In particular, a pseudoscalar condensate spontaneously breaks the ordinary $P/CP$ symmetry and disappears through a continuous thermal phase transition. For regular high-temperature FRG trajectories, the dimensionless mixed coupling tends to zero in the infrared, while its dimensionful counterpart remains finite. Increasing temperature also suppresses the scale evolution of the fermion mass phase. Together, these results clarify the distinct roles of thermal fluctuations in suppressing spontaneous symmetry breaking and fermionic instabilities, without necessarily eliminating explicit symmetry breaking effects.

</details>

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原初电荷与运动诱导动能矩的最小现象学对偶矩框架，统一描述引力、电磁力和强力，并预测了对快速自转致密天体附近光线偏折的可区分修正，同时设计了三种独立检验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一四大基本相互作用是基础物理学的核心未解难题，现有方案多依赖额外维度、新对称性或未观测的动力自由度，本文旨在构建更简洁、现象学上可行的统一框架。

Method: 构建基于原始电荷和运动诱导动能矩的对偶矩框架，引入普适耦合关系 T = Z/c²，将不同相互作用的源电荷耦合系数与矩介导耦合系数相联系，并在逆平方律形式下统一描述引力、电磁力和强力。

Result: 该框架成功复现行星轨道、原子系统和重夸克强子等低能动力学极限，并预测了对快速自转致密天体光线偏折的可观测修正；提出了三种检验方案：改进扭秤测量、实验室质量流偏折实验、天文光线弯曲观测。

Conclusion: 该框架为基本相互作用统一提供了新思路，虽弱相互作用定量计算留待后续，但天文观测（尤其致密自转天体）有望对其形成有力约束或证伪。

Abstract: The unification of the four fundamental interactions remains one of the central open challenges in fundamental physics. Most existing unification scenarios rely on extra spatial dimensions, novel symmetry structures, or unobserved dynamical degrees of freedom. In this work, we construct a minimal phenomenological dual-moment framework rooted in primitive charges and motion-induced kinetic moments. Gravitational, electromagnetic, and strong interactions are coherently described within a unified inverse-square-law formalism. We postulate a universal coupling relation $T = Z/c^2$ that connects source-charge coupling coefficients and moment-mediated coupling coefficients across different interaction sectors. While the framework can formally accommodate weak-charge degrees of freedom, full quantitative computations for weak-interaction dynamics are deferred to future follow-up studies. The model reproduces established low-energy dynamical limits for planetary orbits, atomic systems, and heavy-quark hadrons, and predicts distinguishable corrections to light deflection by rapidly spinning compact objects. We further design three independent test schemes: improved torsion-balance measurements, a mass-current laboratory deflection experiment, and astronomical light-bending observations. Although laboratory measurements impose formidable technical challenges, astronomical observations of compact spinning objects offer promising avenues to constrain or falsify the proposed framework.

</details>

Result: 给出ρ_HC、ω_HC和η′_HC在FCC-hh上通过q_HC强子化产生的预期产额与发现潜力；ρ_HC和η′_HC的强子化信号可与Drell-Yan或VBF直接产生互补，构成希格斯复合性的‘确凿证据’（smoking gun）。

Abstract: We consider a QCD-inspired chiral $SU(4)$ model, with a new fundamental sector ($q_{\rm HC}$) charged under the confining hypercolor gauge group $SU(3)_{\rm HC}$ as a template for electroweak dynamics and compositeness. The $(\mathrm{SU}(4)_L\times\mathrm{SU}(4)_R)/\mathrm{SU}(4)_V$ breaking leads to a rich structure of spin-0 and spin-1 resonances which can be produced by the hadronization of $q_{\rm HC}$ in Drell-Yan processes at FCC-hh. Working in the true vacuum at a general misalignment angle $θ$, the fermion currents are derived for the fifteen techni-rho ($ρ_{\rm HC}$) resonances, the techni-omega ($ω_{\rm HC}$), and the techni-eta-prime ($η^{\prime}_{\rm HC}$) using the Hidden Local Symmetry formalism. The estimated branching fractions pave the way for studying the model spectroscopy. The analysis of the production by hadronization of $ρ_{\rm HC}$, $ω_{\rm HC}$ and $η^{\prime}_{\rm HC}$ states is presented for a 100 TeV future hadronic collider, FCC-hh. The projected discovery of $ρ_{\rm HC}$ and $η^{\prime}_{\rm HC}$ is complementary to the direct production by Drell-Yan or Vector Boson Fusion, providing a smoking gun signature for Higgs compositeness.

Abstract: We study baryogenesis from the post-inflationary decay of helical U(1)$_{L_μ-L_τ}$ gauge fields generated during axion inflation. Assuming that Schwinger pair production is suppressed during inflation, a large helicity-to-entropy ratio $η_H$ can be generated while keeping the initial lepton asymmetry just after inflation negligible. After reheating, the dissipation of magnetic helicity sources a lepton asymmetry through the chiral anomaly, which is partially converted into a baryon asymmetry by electroweak sphaleron processes. We find that there are parameter spaces where the observed baryon asymmetry can be generated in both the turbulent and dissipation-dominated regimes of the U(1)$_{L_μ-L_τ}$ magnetic field evolution. The baryon asymmetry generation in the turbulent regime is, however, dominated by the pre-turbulent nearly frozen regime. We further find that the requirement of successful baryogenesis imposes stronger constraints on the inflationary Hubble parameter and the helicity density generated during axion inflation than the requirement for the gauge-field energy not to backreact on the inflaton dynamics.

Abstract: We show that a scalar whose vacuum expectation value (vev) breaks lepton number at the MeV scale, generating the light-neutrino masses through the inverse seesaw, can also serve as the light mediator of self-interacting dark matter. Dark matter is a pseudo-Dirac fermion whose mass splitting arises from the same vev and is predicted to be proportional to the self-interaction coupling times the vev. A strong central interaction between the dark states requires this splitting to be sizable. The CP-even mediator decays into neutrinos at a rate fixed by the neutrino masses, independent of its mixing with the Higgs boson. Fixing the coupling by the relic abundance and evaluating exact partial-wave cross sections, the model reproduces the velocity dependence of the self-interaction cross section inferred from dwarf galaxies to clusters along a narrow band, $m_s\simeq1.9~{\rm MeV}\times(m_χ/{\rm GeV})$, extending up to $m_χ\simeq12$~GeV with a best fit at $m_χ\simeq7.6$~GeV and $m_s\simeq15$~MeV, and on a sequence of resonant islands of comparable fit quality at higher masses. The cluster data bound the dark matter mass from above, so that the allowed masses extend from about $3$ to $70$~GeV ($95\%$ confidence level), with correlated mediator masses of $6$-$28$~MeV. Combining constraints from the effective number of neutrino species, SN~1987A and direct detection, we find the lepton-number-breaking scale confined to a window of a few hundred MeV and a strong preference for normal neutrino mass ordering. The allowed parameter space is testable by future measurements of $N_{\rm eff}$, low-threshold direct detection and the neutrino mass ordering.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F4和F5结构函数在轻子过程中因轻子质量效应被压制，但在τ子和中微子散射（如SHiP、IceCube、DUNE等实验）中变得重要，需精确计算以支持相关实验分析。

Method: 在Aivazis-Collins-Olness-Tung方案下进行重夸克对F4和F5的次领头阶（NLO）理论计算，使用开源库APFEL++实现，并采用CT18NLO部分子分布函数进行数值评估。

Result: 首次定量给出F4和F5对带电流τ中微子截面的影响，在低Q²和大x的运动学边界附近可造成最高达-40%的抑制。

Conclusion: 重夸克对F4和F5的NLO贡献不可忽略，尤其在τ子和中微子散射实验中，必须纳入以提高理论预测精度。

Abstract: We present next-to-leading-order calculations of heavy-quark contributions to the deep-inelastic scattering structure functions $F_4$ and $F_5$ within the Aivazis-Collins-Olness-Tung scheme, implemented in the open-source library APFEL++ and evaluated using CT18NLO parton distribution functions. These structure functions are suppressed by lepton mass effects in light-lepton processes, but become important in $τ$-lepton and neutrino scattering at facilities such as SHiP, IceCube, and DUNE. We furthermore quantify the direct impact of $F_4$ and $F_5$ on the charged-current $τ$-neutrino cross section, for the first time presented here, showing they can suppress it by up to $-40\%$ near the kinematic boundary at low $Q^2$ and large $x$.

</details>


### [95] [Where does the neutrino Mass Ordering preference come from?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13629)
*Pedro A. N. Machado,João Paulo Pinheiro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了JUNO、Daya Bay与NOvA、T2K、MINOS及IceCube/DeepCore等实验数据联合对中微子质量序的限制，发现当前1.9σ偏好正常序源于多个小效应的平衡，且T2K与NOvA同反应堆数据组合给出相反的质量序倾向；进一步提升JUNO精度无法显著增强对倒置序的排除（上限约3σ），瓶颈在于ν_μ消失实验的能量重建精度，需将中微子-原子核相互作用建模控制在1%水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JUNO最新结果使反应堆反电子中微子消失与加速器μ子中微子消失测得的有效质量劈裂比较成为确定中微子质量序的关键手段，亟需厘清哪些数据集主导当前质量序偏好及其物理根源与系统限制。

Method: 联合分析JUNO和Daya Bay的反应堆中微子数据，以及NOvA、T2K、MINOS和IceCube/DeepCore的ν_μ消失数据，通过统计拟合评估不同质量序的似然偏好，并分解各实验组合的贡献，同时分析未来数据提升的潜力与关键系统误差来源。

Result: 当前联合分析在1.9σ水平上排斥倒置质量序；T2K与NOvA分别同反应堆数据组合时给出相反的质量序倾向，其偏好的Δm²₃₁数值相差约3.5%，接近两序理论差异量级；进一步JUNO数据最多将倒置序排斥提升至约3σ，瓶颈在于ν_μ消失实验的中微子能量重建精度。

Conclusion: 中微子质量序的精确测定已进入系统误差主导阶段，需将中微子-原子核相互作用建模精度提升至1%量级，单靠提升反应堆实验统计精度难以突破当前限制。

Abstract: After the recent JUNO result, the comparison of the effective mass splittings measured in $\barν_e$ disappearance at reactors and in $ν_μ$ disappearance has become one of the leading handles on the neutrino mass ordering. In this paper, we study which data sets drive the present preference for the ordering, combining JUNO and Daya Bay with $ν_μ$ disappearance data from NOvA, T2K, MINOS, and IceCube/DeepCore. While the combination disfavors the inverted ordering at $1.9σ$, this preference comes from a balance between several small preferences. In particular, the combinations of reactor data with T2K or with NOvA point to opposite orderings. The values of the mass splitting preferred by these two experiments differ by about $3.5\%$, comparable to the difference expected between the two orderings, and thus a shift of this size in a single experiment is enough to reverse the preference. Moreover, in the context of this combination of reactor and $ν_μ$ disappearance data, further JUNO data would not bring the rejection of the inverted ordering beyond about $3σ$, as the comparison is then limited by the precision of the $ν_μ$ disappearance experiment. Improving the $ν_μ$ disappearance measurements requires control of the neutrino energy reconstruction, and thus the modeling of neutrino-nucleus interaction physics, at the percent level.

</details>


### [96] [Construction of the $f_4$ meson family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13679)
*Zhe-Qi Huang,Lei Huang,Yu-Qiang Li,Xin-Lei Ge,Hao Chen,Cheng-Qun Pang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过修正的Godfrey-Isgur模型和夸克对产生模型，研究了f4介子家族的质量谱和强衰变特性，支持f4(2300)为非奇异2³F₄态，并预测了奇异2F态及其他高激发态的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受f4(2050)、f4(2300)实验观测及a4家族确立的启发，系统研究f4家族的谱学与衰变行为。

Method: 结合修正Godfrey-Isgur模型计算质量谱，以及夸克对产生模型（QPC）进行两体强衰变计算；以f4(2050)总宽度标定共同对产生强度。

Result: 质量谱与衰变分析均支持f4(2300)为非奇异2³F₄态；预言奇异2F态位于2453 MeV、总宽216 MeV，主衰变道为K* K̄*(1410)和K* K̄*；给出3F、4F、1H、2H等态的预测；H波态宽度约为同味F波态的两倍以上。

Conclusion: 该工作为f4家族的实验确认与后续探测提供了理论依据和关键指导。

Abstract: Motivated by the experimental observations of $f_4(2050)$ and $f_4(2300)$ and the establishment of $a_4$ family, we investigate the $f_4$ family by combining the mass spectrum under the modified Godfrey--Isgur model with two-body strong-decay calculations in the quark-pair-creation model. The common pair-creation strength is fixed by the measured total width of $f_4(2050)$. The mass spectrum analysis and decay information both favor the nonstrange $2\,{}^3F_4$ assignment for $f_4(2300)$. The largely strange $2F$ partner is predicted at $2453$ MeV with a total width of $216$ MeV, decaying mainly into $K^*\bar K^*(1410)$ and $K^*\bar K^*$. Predictions are also given for the $3F$, $4F$, $1H$, and $2H$ flavor pairs. At the adopted mixing angles, the $H$-wave states are more than twice as broad as nearby $F$-wave states of the same flavor. The results of this work may serve as useful guidance for future experimental studies.

</details>


### [97] [Three Dimensional Structure of Pion with One Dynamical Gluon in the Light-Cone Quark Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13805)
*Satyajit Puhan,Shubham Sharma,Narinder Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含动力学胶子的π介子三维结构，通过光前夸克模型计算了广义部分子分布（GPDs）、横动量依赖部分子分布（TMDs）和部分子分布函数（PDFs），并成功满足夸克数和动量求和规则，同时获得电磁形状因子和质量分解；经NLO演化后，PDF结果与Drell-Yan、J/ψ、Υ及HERA领中子F₂^π实验数据符合良好，且q̄qg组分在小x区域和胶子敏感观测量中起重要作用。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解π介子内部结构，特别是超越主导Fock态（|q̄q⟩）的高阶组分（如|q̄qg⟩）对三维结构、胶子贡献及小x物理的影响。

Method: 在光前夸克模型框架下，采用修正的Brodsky-Huang-Lepage波函数（含Melosh-Wigner自旋旋转）描述价态|q̄q⟩，并用光前夸克-胶子顶点处理|q̄qg⟩组分；通过光前波函数重叠计算GPDs、TMDs和PDFs，并进行NLO演化。

Result: 满足夸克数与动量求和规则；给出电磁形状因子和质量分解；PDF与Drell-Yan、J/ψ、Υ及HERA领中子F₂^π数据一致；q̄qg组分对Drell-Yan贡献小，但在小x区域和J/ψ产生等胶子敏感过程中显著。

Conclusion: π介子的|q̄qg⟩组分虽不主导整体动量分布，却对胶子主导过程和小x结构至关重要，凸显多粒子Fock态在强子三维结构研究中的不可忽略作用。

Abstract: We study the three-dimensional structure of the pion beyond the leading Fock state by including a dynamical gluon, $|π\rangle=|q\bar q\rangle+|q\bar q g\rangle$. Within the light-cone quark model, we calculate the pion GPDs, TMDs, and PDFs from light-front wave function overlaps, using a modified Brodsky--Huang--Lepage wave function with the Melosh--Wigner rotation for the valence sector and the light-front quark--gluon vertex for the $q\bar q g$ sector. The quark-number and momentum sum rules are satisfied without additional constraints. We further obtain the electromagnetic form factor and the mass decomposition. After NLO evolution, we compare the PDFs with pion-induced Drell--Yan, $J/ψ$, $Υ$, and HERA leading-neutron $F_2^π$ data. The $q\bar q g$ sector contributes little to Drell--Yan but becomes important for gluon-sensitive observables such as $J/ψ$ production and the pion structure function at small $x$.

</details>


### [98] [Ultra-Heavy Dark Matter Through Exponential Production Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13842)
*Disha Bhatia,Ranjeet Kumar,Rahul Srivastava*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了暗物质的指数产生机制，提出了一种非热产生方式，通过暗物质χ与浴粒子ξ的散射过程χ+ξ→χ+χ实现其丰度的指数增长；研究了Z₃对称性下的标量暗物质模型，并基于可微扰性限制得出暗物质质量上限约为10¹⁵ GeV；最后将该简化模型嵌入到一个UV完备的Dirac型I类跷跷板模型中，使Z₃成为U(1)_{B-L}对称性的残余子群，自然连接中微子物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索热产生之外的暗物质产生机制，特别是指数增长型非热机制，以解释超重暗物质的可能性，并建立与中微子物理的自然联系。

Method: 基于Z₃对称性构建标量暗物质与标准模型单态浴粒子的简化模型，通过分析χ³ξ型有效相互作用和可微扰性约束确定暗物质质量上限；随后将其嵌入UV完备的Dirac型I类跷跷板模型，其中Z₃作为U(1)_{B-L}破缺后的残留对称性。

Result: 得出暗物质质量上限约为O(10¹⁵) GeV（当浴粒子质量与暗物质相近时）；证明该指数产生机制在弱耦合至强耦合区间均可能成立；成功构建了与中微子质量生成机制相容的UV完备模型。

Conclusion: 指数产生机制为超重暗物质提供了可行且现象学上有趣的框架，且可通过残余Z₃对称性自然衔接中微子物理，增强了理论自洽性与可观测潜力。

Abstract: Exponential production of dark matter has been introduced recently as one of the alternatives to the widely studied thermal production of dark matter scenarios. This non-thermal production mechanism leads to the exponential growth in the abundance of the dark matter ($χ$) through its scatterings with the bath particle ($ξ$), viz. $χ+ ξ\to χ+ χ$. In our framework, we consider a scalar dark matter $χ$ charged under $Z_3$ symmetry and bath particle $ξ$ to be standard model singlet and determine the maximum mass of the dark matter using the criteria of perturbativity. We find that dark matter has an upper mass around the ballpark of $\mathcal{O}(10^{15})$ GeV for the cases when the bath particle mass is similar to the dark matter mass. The computations are largely performed with minimal model dependence and will hold in any scenario where the renormalizable couplings driving the exponential growth effectively reduce to $χ^3ξ$ interactions. Thus, in exponential production of dark matter, we have a possibility of an ultra-heavy dark matter with large couplings, making the scenario phenomenologically interesting. Later, we extend the simplified dark matter model to a UV-complete Dirac Type-I seesaw model where the $Z_3$ symmetry is obtained as a residual unbroken subgroup of a global $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry, providing a natural connection with the neutrino sector.

</details>


### [99] [Strong decays of $ψ(3770)$, $ψ(4040)$ and $ψ(4160)$ in four relativistic wave-function representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13915)
*Wen-Yuan Ke,Wan-Li Ju,Tianhong Wang,Qiang Li,Guo-Li Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Salpeter方程和修正的^3P_0模型，利用四种不同波函数表示（φ1–φ4）研究了矢量粲偶素ψ(3770)、ψ(4040)和ψ(4160)的OZI允许强衰变，发现仅φ2能同时符合各态宽度及宽度比实验数据，表明成功波函数需含独立S、P、D波成分，其中P波为主导相对论修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强衰变可直接检验重夸克偶素波函数的相对论结构；需厘清不同相对论波函数表示对强衰变宽度预测的影响，并检验其与实验的一致性。

Method: 采用Salpeter方程结合修正的^3P_0模型，构建四种含不同S/P/D波组合的相对论波函数表示（φ1–φ4）；以ψ(3770)→DD̄宽度固定夸克对产生强度，对其它态做无参预测。

Result: φ2预测ψ(4040)和ψ(4160)总宽度分别为94^{+34}_{-23} MeV和100^{+23}_{-20} MeV，与实验值一致；其宽度比也与数据兼容；其余φ1、φ3、φ4均显著偏离实验（2.5σ至5σ）。φ2提取的混合角（7.92°, 25.8°, 26.3°）与耦合通道模型一致。

Conclusion: 成功的相对论波函数必须包含独立的S、P、D波分量，其中P波提供主导相对论修正；波函数节点结构导致理论预测存在较大不确定性；φ2为最优表示，支持现有强衰变机制与相对论处理的有效性。

Abstract: Strong decays provide a direct test of the relativistic structure of heavy quarkonium wave functions. We study the OZI-allowed hadronic decays of the vector charmonia $ψ(3770)$, $ψ(4040)$ and $ψ(4160)$ within the Salpeter equation, using four relativistic wave-function representations $\varphi_1$--$\varphi_4$ that differ in the $S$-, $P$- and $D$-wave components they retain independently, and the revised ${}^3P_0$ model. The quark-pair creation strength is fixed once, by reproducing the measured $ψ(3770)\to D\bar D$ width, and is not readjusted afterwards, so that the results for the two higher states are parameter-free predictions. The representation $\varphi_2$ predicts $94^{+34}_{-23}$~MeV for $ψ(4040)$ and $100^{+23}_{-20}$~MeV for $ψ(4160)$, in agreement with the measured $84.5\pm12.3$ and $69\pm10$~MeV, and it is the only one whose predicted width ratios are compatible with the data. By contrast, $\varphi_1$ overshoots $ψ(4040)$ by $2.5σ$, $\varphi_3$ gives the largest widths and is disfavoured at the $5σ$ level, and $\varphi_4$, which has no independent $S$-wave component, underestimates $ψ(3770)$ by about $2σ$ and cannot be applied to final states containing vector mesons. These results show that a successful representation must contain independent $S$, $P$ and $D$ components, the $P$-wave terms providing the dominant relativistic correction, while the nodal structure of the wave functions accounts for the large uncertainties of the predictions. The mixing angles extracted from $\varphi_2$, $7.92^{\circ}$, $25.8^{\circ}$ and $26.3^{\circ}$, agree with the values obtained in coupled-channel models.

</details>


### [100] [The $χ_{c1}(3872)$ Multiplet in the Diabatic Dynamical Diquark Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14251)
*Richard F. Lebed,Ethan H. Pham*

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展二介子阈值和重夸克自旋对称性约束的二夸克-反二夸克模型，成功解释了χc1(3872)、Tcc1(3900)和Tcc1(4020)等隐藏粲四夸克态，并预测了Σg+(1S)多重态其余成员的位置，发现绝热精细结构在耦合通道中仍基本保持。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释实验上已确认的多个隐藏粲四夸克态（如χc1(3872)、Tcc1(3900)、Tcc1(4020)），原最小二绝热动力学二夸克模型被发现不足，需引入更完整的自旋与同位旋依赖相互作用。

Method: 采用完整形式的绝热-非绝热混合二夸克模型，将原始绝热模型中的自旋与同位旋依赖二夸克-反二夸克相互作用纳入全二绝热势，并结合耦合通道散射模拟进行谱预测。

Result: 模型成功拟合三个已知态；预测Σg+(1S)多重态其余成员质量位置与纯绝热本征质量偏差约10 MeV；表明绝热精细结构在含显式二介子通道时依然稳健。

Conclusion: 完整二绝热模型不仅可统一描述关键实验态，还揭示了隐藏粲四夸克谱中绝热近似仍具强鲁棒性；所预测的新态为未来实验提供了明确搜寻目标。

Abstract: We apply a minimal form of the diabatic dynamical diquark model, which uses only dimeson thresholds augmented by constraints obtained from heavy-quark spin symmetry, to the hidden-charm tetraquark spectrum. We find that this model is insufficient to simultaneously reproduce the experimentally established states \(χ_{c1}(3872)\), \(T_{c\bar c1}(3900)\), and \(T_{c\bar c1}(4020)\). We therefore extend the model to its complete form, by restoring the spin- and isospin-dependent diquark-antidiquark interactions from the original adiabatic dynamical diquark model into the full diabatic potential. Aligning the resulting framework to these three states allows the remaining members of the \(Σ_g^+(1S)\) multiplet to be predicted through coupled-channel scattering simulations. The resulting resonance or bound state locations lie within approximately \(10~\mathrm{MeV}\) of the corresponding pure adiabatic mass eigenvalue predictions in all cases, indicating that the adiabatic fine-structure pattern survives the inclusion of explicit dimeson channels. We also discuss possible experimental counterparts for the unconfirmed multiplet members.

</details>


### [101] [Resolved $Q_1^q$-$Q_{7γ}$ interference in $\bar{B}\to X_sγ$ at NLO: Hard matching and radiative charm-loop jet function for $m_c \to 0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14318)
*Riccardo Bartocci,Philipp Böer,Kevin Brune,Tobias Hurth*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准模型中B介子辐射衰变$\bar{B}\to X_sγ$过程中，由电流-电流算符$Q_1^q$与电磁偶极算符$Q_{7γ}$干涉引起的次领头阶（subleading-power）分辨光子贡献的NLO QCD修正，利用软共线有效理论（SCET）在端点区域构建因子化定理，首次完整给出了质量为零的粲夸克极限下该贡献的所有未知NLO微扰成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该次领头阶分辨光子贡献是当前$\bar{B}\to X_sγ$衰变率理论预测中主要的不确定性来源之一，亟需更高精度的QCD修正以提升理论预言精度。

Method: 基于软共线有效理论（SCET）在动量端点区域（$m_b-2E_γ=\mathcal{O}(Λ_{\rm QCD})$）建立因子化定理；计算$m_c\to0$极限下的单圈无粲硬匹配系数及其重整化群方程，并用$1/N_c$展开前两项给出解析近似解；计算含全动量分数依赖的两圈辐射粲圈喷注函数；结合已知的$\mathcal{O}(α_s)$夸克喷注函数修正和次领头形状函数$g_{17}$的一圈重整化结果。

Result: 首次在$m_c\to0$极限下完成了该次领头阶分辨光子贡献的完整NLO QCD描述；明确了剩余待计算的关键项为辐射粲圈喷注函数对$m_c$的依赖性。

Conclusion: 本工作显著降低了$\bar{B}\to X_sγ$衰变率理论预测中的系统不确定性，为未来结合$m_c$依赖性的唯象分析及高精度检验标准模型奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We study next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD corrections to the subleading-power resolved-photon contribution to $\bar{B}\to X_sγ$ arising from the interference of the current-current operator $Q_1^q$ and the electromagnetic dipole operator $Q_{7γ}$. This contribution is among the main sources of theoretical uncertainty in Standard Model predictions for the decay rate. Using a factorisation theorem in soft-collinear effective theory for the kinematic endpoint region, $m_b-2E_γ=\mathcal{O}(Λ_{\rm QCD})$, we determine all previously unknown perturbative ingredients at NLO in the limit of a vanishing charm-quark mass, $m_c\to0$. Specifically, we compute the $m_c$-independent one-loop hard-matching coefficients, derive their renormalisation-group equations, and obtain accurate analytic approximations to their solutions by retaining the first two terms in a $1/N_c$ expansion. Our second main result is the two-loop correction to the radiative charm-loop jet function, including its full dependence on the collinear momentum fraction. Combining these results with the known $\mathcal{O}(α_s)$ correction to the quark jet function and the one-loop renormalisation of the subleading shape function $g_{17}$, previously computed by some of us, we provide the first complete NLO description of this resolved contribution in the massless-charm limit. This leaves the $m_c$ dependence of the radiative charm-loop jet function as the remaining ingredient for a phenomenological analysis within renormalisation-group-improved perturbation theory.

</details>


### [102] [Renormalization-group flow of nucleon--$Δ$ contact interactions with an explicit $Δ$: decoupling and the scale dependence of large-$N_c$ relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14351)
*Dominador F. Vaso,,Daris Samart,Jingle B. Magallanes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了核子与Δ重子之间在Wilsonian重整化群流下的领头阶接触相互作用，保持Δ作为显式耦合通道，并确定哪些大N_c关系在流下保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解核子-Δ系统中大N_c展开下的对称性与不变性，以及这些关系如何受重整化群流影响。

Method: 采用带尖锐动量截断的Wilsonian重整化群方法，精确求解双通道流；结合1/N_c展开的Hartree哈密顿量分析，并考虑时间反演对称性。

Result: 发现8个自旋-同位旋翻转关系是流的精确不变量；LO固定了18个S波耦合中的11个；exact-SU(4)两参数形式与LO相差1/N_c²阶；ΔΔ散射中(S,I)=(3,0)与(0,3)通道的散射长度在LO相等。

Conclusion: 大N_c关系约束势能在某一尺度上的形式，但不能消除由ΔΔ闭通道动量κ_ΔΔ控制的跑动效应；相关预言可由重π质量下的格点QCD检验。

Abstract: We study the leading-order (LO) contact interactions among nucleons and $Δ$ isobars under a Wilsonian renormalization-group flow with a sharp momentum cutoff, keeping the $Δ$ as an explicit coupled channel, and determine which large-$N_c$ relations among the couplings are invariant under the flow. The two-channel flow with the $ΔΔ$ threshold inside the cutoff range is solved exactly, and the closed channels decouple with relative corrections of order $Λ^2/κ_{ΔΔ}^2$, where $κ_{ΔΔ}=\sqrt{2M_Δ(M_Δ-M_N)}$ is the closed-channel momentum. Applying the $1/N_c$ expansion of the Hartree Hamiltonian to the $N$--$Δ$ system, we find that a generalized time reversal removes all corrections of relative order $1/N_c$, that the LO fixes eleven of the 18 S-wave couplings in terms of seven, and that the exact-SU(4) two-parameter form used in the literature differs from the LO by an operator of relative order $1/N_c^2$ with matrix elements of order one in the $Δ$ channels. The eight spin--isospin flip relations are exact invariants of the flow, also when the central part of one-pion exchange is iterated in the S waves. The eigenchannel relations of the $NN$--$ΔΔ$ sectors hold only at the scale at which they are imposed. Their running is controlled by $κ_{ΔΔ}$, which is of order $N_c^0$, and by a parameter that the $NN$ data do not fix, so that the $1/N_c$ expansion does not remove it. Large-$N_c$ relations between open and closed channels therefore constrain the potential at one scale. The flip relation between the $(S,I)=(3,0)$ and $(0,3)$ channels of $ΔΔ$ scattering predicts equal scattering lengths at LO, which lattice QCD at heavy pion masses can test.

</details>


### [103] [The Higgs boson, naturalness, and the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14370)
*Mikhail Shaposhnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了希格斯玻色子在粒子物理与宇宙学中的多重作用，涵盖自然性问题、真空稳定性、希格斯暴胀、重加热、电弱相变及电弱重子生成等关键议题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子作为唯一已知的基本标量粒子，其在早期宇宙演化和基本粒子理论中可能具有深远影响，亟需系统梳理其跨领域角色。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，整合粒子物理与宇宙学两方面理论进展，分析希格斯场在不同时空尺度下的潜在物理效应。

Result: 厘清了希格斯场在解决自然性与等级问题、维持真空亚稳态、驱动暴胀与重加热、引发电弱相变及实现电弱重子生成等方面的机制与挑战。

Conclusion: 希格斯场不仅是标准模型的核心组分，更可能是连接粒子物理与宇宙演化的关键桥梁，未来实验与理论需协同探索其更深层含义。

Abstract: The Higgs boson -- the only known fundamental scalar particle -- may have profound implications for both particle physics and cosmology. This review examines how the Higgs field may have influenced the Universe from its earliest moments to its present state. We first discuss particle-physics puzzles, including the naturalness and hierarchy problems, as well as the possible metastability of the electroweak vacuum. We then turn to cosmological scenarios where the Higgs boson could be a driving force, including Higgs inflation, reheating and the onset of the hot Big Bang, the electroweak phase transition, and baryogenesis via electroweak processes.

</details>


### [104] [The two-component picture of soft hadron production: Regge exchange, duality, and resonance cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14388)
*Ondrej Chvala*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了软强子碰撞中极限碎裂现象的理论发展，强调基于强子自由度（而非微扰QCD）的两组分级联模型（如HEART），并指出可区分共振级联与弦模型的关键实验观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证极限碎裂现象是否可用前QCD时代的强子物理工具（如Regge理论、共振态、双重性等）充分描述，而非必须依赖量子色动力学（QCD）微扰或弦模型。

Method: 系统梳理自1969年以来的理论框架，包括Regge交换、有限能量求和规则、Harari-Freund二重性、火球/新星/簇等早期级联思想，并整合为事件级强子激发与共振跃迁（HEART）模型。

Result: 提出HEART级联模型作为软碎裂区域的强子化描述；指出可通过前向重子同位旋、伴随产生中的局域奇异数平衡及前向共振信号及其质量尾等实验观测来区分共振级联与弦机制。

Conclusion: 前QCD强子物理工具仍足以刻画软非微扰QCD区域的碎裂行为；极限碎裂本质上可能源于强子激发与共振衰变的级联过程，而非弦断裂。

Abstract: The fragmentation regions of soft hadronic collisions approach energy-independent limits. Benecke, Chou, Yang, and Yen stated this hypothesis of limiting fragmentation in 1969. Feynman gave it a parton-model formulation the same year. Measurements from the CERN Intersecting Storage Rings (ISR) to the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) still show this approach. Fischer and co-workers interpret the NA49 spectra from the CERN Super Proton Synchrotron (SPS) as the sum of a target and a projectile component, each the decay of an excited hadronic system. This review traces the theory behind that two-component picture. It examines the hypothesis that the hadronic tools that predate quantum chromodynamics (QCD) suffice for the soft, non-perturbative domain of QCD. Regge exchange organizes the fragmentation region in triple-Regge form. It supplies the diffractive mass law of the excited systems, with the shrinkage of the Pomeron in its transfer dependence. Finite-energy sum rules and the two-component duality of Harari and Freund equate averaged towers of resonances with that exchange. Duality therefore motivates describing the excited systems in hadronic degrees of freedom. The review follows the cascade description through its predecessors, namely the fireballs of Niu, the novas of Jacob and Slansky, the clusters of Hwa, Good-Walker diffraction, and the two-stage dissociation of Hama. HEART (Hadronic Excitations And Resonance Transitions) assembles these pieces into an event-level cascade of two-body hadronic transitions. A companion paper (arXiv:2611.11570) defines it and tests it against the NA49 data. The review closes with the fragmentation-region measurements that would separate a resonance cascade from a string, namely the isospin of forward baryons, the local balance of strangeness in associated production, and forward resonance signals with their mass tails.

</details>


### [105] [Running out of Field Range: Nonlinear Sourcing of Light Scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14473)
*Abhishek Banerjee,Shourya Mukherjee,Stefan Stelzl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了轻标量场与标准模型线性耦合的实验限制，指出当标量场为赝Nambu-Goldstone玻色子且场值达到其衰变常数f量级时，传统线性耦合假设失效；由此导致空间实验限制减弱数个数量级，而地面实验在低质量区几乎完全失去灵敏度，从而在参数空间中打开多个依赖于f的新可探测窗口，尤其在核子耦合情形下存在迄今未被任何实验覆盖的宽广参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等效原理检验、第五力搜寻、原子钟比对和恒星冷却等实验对轻标量场的限制均基于线性耦合假设，但该假设在赝Nambu-Goldstone玻色子场值达O(f)时失效，需重新评估实验约束。

Method: 针对光子、电子和核子三种耦合，系统重估等效原理实验、第五力实验、原子钟比对及恒星冷却等多类实验的灵敏度，考虑非线性场动力学（如场饱和效应与位置依赖的耦合压制）。

Result: 空间实验限制减弱数个数量级，地面实验在低质量区灵敏度急剧下降；对核子耦合，在质量高于~10^{-12} eV、耦合强度介于‘朴素极限’与恒星冷却限之间的超宽参数区间（逾十个数量级）尚无任何实验约束。

Conclusion: 存在多个f依赖的新可探测窗口，特别是地面-空间原子钟比对实验有望首次探测此前完全未被覆盖的参数区域。

Abstract: Light scalar fields coupled linearly to the Standard Model are constrained by equivalence-principle tests, fifth-force searches, clock comparison tests, and stellar cooling. These bounds assume that the coupling to ordinary matter can be expanded to linear order for all field values reached. For a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson with decay constant $f$ this fails as soon as the field value reaches $O(f)$. The field around a source then stops growing with the coupling, and in addition the linear coupling of a probe is suppressed depending on its location. We reanalyze all of these bounds for the couplings to photons, electrons and nucleons. Space-based tests are weakened by several orders of magnitude, whereas the terrestrial tests are suppressed far more strongly and lose their sensitivity at low masses. As a result, for each coupling, $f$-dependent windows in the parameter space open up. For a coupling to nucleons a window persists at large couplings even for $f\sim 100 \,\rm{GeV}$. In particular, no current experiment constrains couplings between the naive limit and the stellar-cooling bound in a window of masses above $\text{few}\times 10^{-12}\,$eV, a range of more than ten orders of magnitude in coupling. This presents a unique opportunity for a ground-space clock comparison test to probe uncharted territory.

</details>

*Olivia R Pereira,Julie Hlavacek-Larrondo,Michael Reefe,Gary J Ferland,Hyunseop Choi,Mathieu Marquis,Jorge Barrera-Ballesteros,Benjamin Vigneron,Ming Sun,Rebecca E A Canning,Gregory Taylor,Loic Albert,Francesco D'Eugenio,Megan Donahue,Andrew C Fabian,John S Gallagher,Marie-Lou Gendron-Marsolais,Pierre Guillard,Minghao Guo,Nina Hatch,Ralf Kotulla,Yuan Li,Roberto Maiolino,Allison Man,Michael A McDonald,Brian R McNamara,Valeria Olivares,Marine Prunier,Christopher S Reynolds,Carter Rhea,Annabelle Richard-Laferriere,Helen R Russell,Philippe Salome,Prathamesh Tamhane,Auriane Thilloy,Grant R Tremblay,G Mark Voit,Stephen A Walker*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/NIRSpec对Centaurus星系团中心BCG NGC 4696进行空间分辨近红外积分视场光谱观测，揭示了核区、环核旋涡和丝状结构中暖分子气体（H₂）激发条件的显著差异，表明传统光学谱线比无法分辨这些差异，而新的近红外诊断图可有效区分多相冷却-反馈过程中的不同加热机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系团冷却流中ICM、冷分子气体与AGN反馈之间的能量交换机制，而暖分子和电离气体是关键探针；但光学谱线比普遍给出相似的LINER分类，难以分辨不同结构中的分子激发差异。

Method: 采用JWST/NIRSpec对NGC 4696中心3″×3″区域开展积分视场光谱观测，分析H I和H₂转动-振动发射线，构建H₂激发图和空间分辨的近红外诊断图，并对比光学诊断图。

Result: H₂激发温度为~1800–2200 K，高能级显示需更热或非热成分；丝状区H₂ 1-0 S(1)/Paα比核区高4–8倍；光学诊断图无法区分区域，而新提出的近红外诊断图可清晰分辨核、旋涡与丝状结构的激发差异；结果与AGN光电离、ICM冷却辐射及非辐射加热共同作用一致。

Conclusion: 空间分辨近红外光谱能揭示被传统光学分类掩盖的暖分子气体激发变化，为约束星系团核心多相冷却-反馈循环提供新途径。

Abstract: The warm molecular and ionized gas in filamentary nebulae of cool-core BCGs provide a sensitive probe of the poorly understood exchange of energy between the ICM, cold molecular gas, and AGN feedback. Optical line ratios commonly yield similar LINER-like classifications across these systems, but do not resolve how the excitation of warm molecular gas varies between compact nuclear structures and surrounding filaments. We present JWST/NIRSpec integral-field spectroscopy of the central $3''\times3''$ ($620\times620~{\rm pc^2}$) of the Centaurus cluster BCG, NGC~4696. The NIR data resolve H$I$ and ro-vibrational H$_2$ emission across compact nuclear apertures, a circumnuclear swirl, and filamentary structures. H$_2$ excitation diagrams yield characteristic low-excitation temperatures of $T_{\rm ex}\simeq1.8-2.2\times10^3$~K, while curvature at higher energies requires hotter or non-thermal components. The H$_2$/Pa$α$ balance varies strongly: filamentary regions have H$_2$ 1-0 S(1)/Pa$α$ ratios 4-8 times higher than the compact nuclear apertures. In contrast, matched-aperture optical diagnostic diagrams show substantially less regional separation, with all regions occupying a low-ionization locus. We thus introduce new spatially resolved NIR diagnostic diagrams that reveal distinct molecular excitation conditions across the nucleus, swirl, and filaments. Ratios are broadly consistent with contributions from AGN-related photoionization, ICM-related cooling radiation, and non-radiative heating, although the present data do not uniquely determine their relative importance. These results demonstrate how spatially resolved NIR spectroscopy reveals excitation variations hidden in conventional optical classifications, providing a new route to constrain the multiphase cooling-feedback cycle in cluster cores.

</details>


### [107] [Dust Reverberation Mapping of Low-Mass Active Galactic Nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13181)
*Chi-Zhuo Wang,Hengxiao Guo,Xue-Bing Wu,Wenwen Zuo,Wanling Liu,Jingbo Sun,Wenke Ren,Shu Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究对低质量活动星系核（AGN）进行了系统的尘埃反响映射分析，利用ZTF和WISE数据测得W1/W2波段滞后，并建立了跨越七个数量级光度的统一R_dust-L_AGN关系，发现其斜率略低于理论预期，并探讨了Eddington比和SED变化对其的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低质量AGN中尘埃环的结构与辐射机制，检验R_dust-L_AGN关系在宽光度范围内的普适性及偏离理论斜率0.5的原因。

Method: 基于文献编目选取低质量AGN样本，结合ZTF光学与WISE W1/W2红外光变数据进行尘埃反响映射分析；通过质量筛选获得65个可靠滞后测量源；联合高光度AGN样本构建统一R_dust-L_AGN关系；分析滞后偏差与Eddington比、SPHEREx复合谱SED变化的关联。

Result: W2滞后普遍长于W1，中值比τ_W2/τ_W1=1.24^{+0.33}_{-0.21}；统一R_dust-L_AGN关系斜率为0.47±0.01（W1）和0.44±0.01（W2），略低于0.5；滞后偏差与Eddington比呈负相关；SPHEREx显示光度依赖的SED变化，表明常用光度示踪可能不完全反映尘埃加热光度。

Conclusion: R_dust-L_AGN关系斜率偏浅可能源于 slim disk 的自遮蔽效应及SED变化导致的光度代理偏差；未来Rubin LSST与Roman望远镜联合监测将拓展至更低光度与黑洞质量的AGN。

Abstract: We perform a systematic dust reverberation mapping study of low-mass ($M_{\mathrm{BH}}\sim10^6$-$10^7 M_\odot$) active galactic nuclei (AGNs) selected from a literature-compiled catalog, using data from the Zwicky Transient Facility and the Wide-field Infrared Survey Explorer. Through quality assessments, we obtain a core sample of 65 sources with W1- and W2-band lag measurements relative to optical light curves. The W2-band lags are generally longer than the W1-band lags, with a median ratio of $τ_{\mathrm{W2}}/τ_{\mathrm{W1}}=1.24^{+0.33}_{-0.21}$. We further investigate the relations between dust radius and AGN luminosity ($R_{\mathrm{dust}}-L_{\mathrm{AGN}}$), using different lag measurements and luminosity proxies. Combining our sample with a higher-luminosity AGN sample, we establish unified $R_{\mathrm{dust}}-L_{\mathrm{AGN}}$ relations spanning nearly seven orders of magnitude in bolometric luminosity. The slopes are $0.47\pm0.01$ and $0.44\pm0.01$ for the W1 and W2 bands, slightly below the expectation of $0.5$ from dust radiative equilibrium. In the combined sample, the lag deviations show a negative correlation with Eddington ratio, potentially related to the self-shadowing effect of slim disks. The luminosity-dependent SED variations revealed by the SPHEREx composite spectra suggest that individual luminosity proxies may not fully trace the intrinsic dust-heating luminosity. Both effects may contribute to the shallow $R_{\mathrm{dust}}-L_{\mathrm{AGN}}$ slopes. Future joint optical and near-infrared monitoring with the Vera C. Rubin Observatory's Legacy Survey of Space and Time and the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope will further extend dust RM studies to lower-luminosity AGNs hosting lower-mass black holes.

</details>


### [108] [Giant Radio Galaxies from the Third Data Release of the LOFAR Two-metre Sky Survey (LoTSS-DR3): Physical Characteristics and Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13185)
*Souvik Manik,Netai Bhukta,Sabyasachi Pal,Sushanta K. Mondal*

TL;DR: 本文基于LOFAR巡天数据DR3，构建了2333个巨型射电星系（GRG）样本，系统分析其射电性质、喷流能量学、黑洞质量、红外颜色及环境特征，发现射电瓣压强随尺度减小但随红移升高，并揭示高红移GRG在宇宙早期演化研究中的潜力与观测限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巨型射电星系（GRGs）是研究大尺度喷流演化及其环境的关键探针，尤其高红移样本对理解早期宇宙中AGN反馈和结构形成具有重要意义；然而此前缺乏大样本、高红移的GRG统计研究。

Method: 基于LOFAR Two-metre Sky Survey Data Release 3（LoTSS-DR3）识别并编目2333个GRG，测量其投影尺寸、红移、150 MHz光度；结合多波段数据（WISE红外测光、X射线星系团目录等）分析射电瓣能量学（equipartition压力）、黑洞质量估算、宿主星系红外颜色分类及环境（如BCG与星系团质量关系）。

Result: 发现：（1）约31% GRG位于z≥1；（2）equipartition压力随投影尺寸减小、但随红移升高（该趋势在控制射电光度和尺寸后仍显著）；（3）~75%有WISE测光的GRG落入AGN/HERG区域；（4）BCG寄主的GRG物理尺寸不依赖星系团质量，归一化半径D/(2r₅₀₀)随团质量下降仅反映r₅₀₀的自相似缩放；（5）高红移GRG受面亮度变暗和CMB逆康普顿损耗影响，探测受限。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了迄今最大、红移最高的GRG样本，证实其在宇宙学尺度上的多样性与演化特征，支持GRG生长主要受喷流动力学与环境压强平衡主导，而非被高密度星系团环境显著抑制；同时强调未来低频、高灵敏度巡天对突破高红移GRG探测极限的重要性。

Abstract: Giant radio galaxies (GRGs) are radio-loud active galactic nuclei (AGN) with projected linear sizes exceeding 0.7 Mpc, offering insights into the evolution of large-scale radio jets and their environments. We present 2333 GRGs from the LOFAR Two-metre Sky Survey Data Release 3 (LoTSS-DR3), with projected sizes of 0.7-2.9 Mpc, redshifts of $0.126 \leq z \leq 3.517$, and 150-MHz radio luminosities of $1.72 \times 10^{24}$-$3.04 \times 10^{28}$ W Hz$^{-1}$. Of these, 726 ($\sim 31\%$) lie at $z \geq 1$. We investigate their radio properties, lobe energetics, equipartition pressures, black hole masses, mid-infrared colours, and environments. Equipartition pressure decreases with increasing projected size, consistent with declining lobe energy density as radio structures expand, while its positive correlation with redshift persists after controlling for radio power and projected size. WISE colours highlight the diversity of the host-galaxy population, with approximately 75% of sources with available photometry falling within the empirical AGN/HERG region. For BCG-hosted GRGs, physical projected size shows no significant dependence on cluster mass. Although the normalized projected half-size, $D/(2r_{500})$, decreases with increasing cluster mass, this trend is consistent with the expected self-similar scaling of cluster radius rather than an independent suppression of radio-source growth in massive clusters. The substantial high-redshift population provides an opportunity to investigate GRGs at early cosmic epochs, although surface-brightness dimming and inverse-Compton losses against the cosmic microwave background can limit their detectability.

</details>


### [109] [TITAN DR1: The Type Ia Supernova Trove from ATLAS in the Nearby Universe - Host Galaxies I: Associations, Redshifts and Derived Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13195)
*Jack W. Tweddle,Yukei S. Murakami,Maria Vincenzi,Stephen J. Smartt,Bastien Carreres,Fiorenzo Stoppa,Erik R. Peterson,Brodie Popovic,Jasper Milstein,MaKenzie Elliott,Elijah G. Marlin,Dillon Brout,David O. Jones,Mitchell Dixon,Adam G. Riess,Daniel M. Scolnic,Alejandro Clocchiatti*
