TL;DR: 本文介绍了TITAN DR1数据集的宿主星系目录，包含8378颗低红移Ia型超新星（z ≲ 0.15）及其宿主星系的系统性测量与物理性质推导，是目前最大、最均匀的低红移SN Ia样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个大样本、高一致性、多波段覆盖的低红移Ia型超新星宿主星系目录，以支撑超新星光度校准、环境依赖性研究及宇宙学应用。

Method: 结合四种宿主匹配方法并辅以人工检验；获取光谱红移并进行CMB帧与本动速度修正；进行PSF匹配的FUV–MIR测光（含全局与SN位置1.5 kpc内局部测光）；使用Bagpipes工具开展SED拟合以推导恒星质量、星族年龄、尘埃消光、SFR等物理量。

Result: 成功识别7856颗（93.8%）超新星的可靠宿主；获得5522个宿主的光谱红移；7568个宿主完成PSF匹配测光；全部宿主获得全局SED拟合结果，7329个获得局部拟合；5827个宿主获得可靠的SFR、AV和质量加权年龄估计；宿主星系具有代表性低红移星系分布特征（中位恒星质量log(M*/M⊙)=10.22，71%为恒星形成星系）。

Conclusion: 该宿主星系目录为研究Ia型超新星光度步长（luminosity step）、宿主环境效应及提升其作为宇宙学标准烛光的精度提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present the host-galaxy catalogue for TITAN DR1, a sample of 8378 spectroscopically confirmed Type Ia supernovae (SNe~Ia) at $z \lesssim 0.15$ -- the largest homogeneous low-redshift SN Ia sample assembled to date. We describe the association of each SN with its host, the compilation of redshifts, measurements of uniform far-ultraviolet to mid-infrared (FUV--MIR) photometry, and the derivation of host properties through spectral energy distribution (SED) fitting. Combining four host-association methods with manual vetting, we securely identify hosts for 7856 SNe~Ia (93.8\%). We compile host-galaxy spectroscopic redshifts for 5522 objects and provide CMB-frame and peculiar-velocity-corrected redshifts. \rev{PSF-matched} photometry is measured for 7568 host galaxies, including both global fluxes and local optical-only fluxes within a fixed 1.5~kpc aperture centred on the SN; \texttt{Bagpipes} returns successful global fits for \rev{all} hosts and local fits for 7329. Stellar mass and rest-frame $g-z$ colour are robustly measured from optical data alone, whereas reliable estimates of star-formation rate, dust attenuation, and mass-weighted age require broader wavelength coverage to break age--dust degeneracies\rev{, and are reported for 5827 hosts}; in our redshift range, MIR data suffice and far-IR coverage is not required. The hosts span a representative low-redshift galaxy population, with a median stellar mass of $\log_{10}(M_*/M_\odot)=10.22$ (\rev{60\%} high-mass; $\log_{10}(M_*/M_\odot)>10$), \rev{71\%} star-forming ($\log_{10}(\mathrm{sSFR}/\mathrm{yr}^{-1}) > -10.5$), and an approximately exponential $A_V$ distribution. This catalogue provides the foundation for forthcoming analyses of the luminosity step and the broader role of galaxy environment in SN~Ia standardization and cosmology.

</details>


### [110] [Intermediate-redshift slow rotators as the progenitors of the most massive galaxies at z $\sim$ 0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13225)
*C. Muñoz López,Davor Krajnović,B. Epinat,T. Contini,I. Pessa,J. Pharo,A. de Araujo Carvalho,A. R. Chougule*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了218个中等红移（0.1 < z < 1）星系的恒星运动学与恒星形成历史及其环境依赖性，发现约8%为慢速旋转星系，其角动量损失机制与末次恒星形成事件的关系尚不明确，且它们可能是本地宇宙中大质量慢速旋转星系的前身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中等红移星系的恒星运动学特性、恒星形成历史及其与环境（如密度）的关系，以揭示星系演化路径，特别是慢速旋转星系的起源与演化。

Method: 利用VLT/MUSE在CANDELS和COSMOS天区观测218个0.1 < z < 1的星系，测量其恒星运动学（特别是自旋参数λ_R）和恒星形成历史；结合已有文献数据，并按红移（z < 0.6 / z > 0.6）、环境密度和形态分类分析。

Result: 发现约8%（17/218）为慢速旋转星系；z < 0.6子样本更接近本地星系性质；z > 0.6存在运动学形态-密度关系；慢速旋转星系多经历一至两次主恒星形成事件并在z ~ 1时停止恒星形成；其质量-尺寸关系支持其为本地大质量慢速旋转星系的前身。

Conclusion: 中等红移慢速旋转星系很可能是本地大质量慢速旋转星系的前身，后续通过干并合（gas-poor mergers）增长；但角动量损失是否与末次恒星形成直接相关仍不确定。

Abstract: We study the resolved stellar kinematics and star formation histories, and their dependence on the environment, in a sample of 218 intermediate redshift (0.1 < z < 1) galaxies located in the CANDELS and COSMOS fields, observed with VLT/MUSE. The sample covers galaxies with stellar masses ranging M_star ~10^8 - 10^12 M_sun, including both star-forming and quiescent systems. For 113 galaxies, found in dense environments of galaxy groups, we derive the spin parameter ($λ_{R}$), while for the rest of the sample, we use data from previous papers in this series. Given that the surveys used in this work are flux-limited, the $λ_{R}$ distribution of our full sample lacks the bimodality observed in the local Universe, but the subsample of galaxies at z < 0.6 resembles the galaxy population in the nearby Universe, in terms of mass, sizes, and stellar population properties, more than galaxies at z > 0.6. We classified the galaxies into fast and slow rotators, finding that ~8% (17/218) of the galaxies are slow-rotating. The fraction of slow rotators does not change significantly down to z = 0, but we find that in the z ~ 0 sample, the spin parameter is, on average, higher than at higher redshifts. In our subsample at z > 0.6, we observed evidence for the existence of the kinematic morphology-density relation. The slow rotators in our sample mostly assemble their stellar mass in one or two main episodes of formation, and they finished assembling their mass via star formation by redshift of z $\sim$ 1. It is however, not clear if the loss of angular momentum can be associated with the last star formation episode. Based on their location in the mass - size diagram, the intermediate-redshift galaxies are consistent with being the progenitors of the most massive slow rotators observed at z~0, where the subsequent growth is facilitated through gas-poor (dry) mergers.

</details>


### [111] [The UV luminosity function at 1.6 < z < 2.2 from UVCANDELS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13518)
*Lei Sun,Shamuhawu Hailanhazi,Xin Wang,Harry I. Teplitz,Vihang Mehta,Anahita Alavi,Christopher J. Conselice,Matthew Hayes,Ray A. Lucas,Marc Rafelski,Brian Siana,Rogier A. Windhorst,L. Y. Aaron Yung,Norman Grogin,Ben Sunnquist*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用UVCANDELS F435W数据，分析了红移1.6–2.2范围内星系的紫外光度函数（LF），得到斜率α≈−1.59，并探讨其随红移的演化及对紫外光度密度ρ_UV的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定高红移星系紫外光度函数，理解星系形成与演化，特别是紫外光度密度ρ_UV的红移演化趋势。

Method: 基于UVCANDELS F435W成像数据，构建z=1.6–2.2、M_UV≤−16.8的5212个星系样本；采用最大似然法拟合Schechter函数；分红移子样本分析参数变化；结合F275W及其他文献数据建模ρ_UV的z=0–10演化。

Result: 测得z∼1.9处LF的 faint-end 斜率α=−1.586±0.042；z∼1.75和z∼2.05处α分别为−1.488±0.055和−1.604±0.066（差异约1.4σ）；M*显著变亮（~2.3σ）；ρ_UV=(1.821±0.037)×10²⁶ erg s⁻¹ Hz⁻¹ Mpc⁻³；并给出z=0–10的ρ_UV演化模型。

Conclusion: 紫外光度函数在z∼1.6–2.2区间内呈现轻微但可测的演化，faint-end斜率变陡、特征光度变亮，表明小质量星系对紫外辐射贡献增强；所建ρ_UV红移演化模型为再电离和恒星形成历史研究提供关键约束。

Abstract: We present an analysis of the rest-frame UV luminosity function (LF) of galaxies at $1.6<z<2.2$ using the UVCANDELS F435W imaging data. Restricting our analysis to sources above $30\%$ completeness to minimize uncertainties at the faint end, we construct a catalog of 5,212 galaxies at $1.6<z<2.2$ down to an absolute magnitude of $M_{\rm UV} = -16.8$, using well-tested photometric redshifts. We perform a maximum likelihood estimate to derive the best-fit Schechter parameters of the UV LF at a median redshift of $z\sim1.9$. We report a best-fit faint-end slope of $α= -1.586 \pm 0.042$. Splitting the sample into two redshift bins, we obtain $α=-1.488 \pm 0.055$ at $z\sim1.75$ and $α=-1.604 \pm 0.066$ at $z\sim2.05$ ($\sim1.4σ$ difference), alongside a $\sim2.3σ$ brightening in $M^{*}$. Integrating our best-fit LF down to $M_{\rm UV} = -15$, we derive $ρ_{\rm UV} = (1.821 \pm 0.037) \times 10^{26}\ \rm erg\ s^{-1}\ Hz^{-1}\ Mpc^{-3}$ at $z\sim1.9$. Combining our F435W measurements with previous UVCANDELS F275W results and other literature data, we model the redshift evolution of $ρ_{\rm UV}$ from $z\sim0$ to $z\sim10$.

</details>


### [112] [Molecular signatures of shocked gas and enhanced cosmic-ray ionization in supernova remnant W49B revealed by ALMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13519)
*Wenjin Yang,Ping Zhou,Zhi-Yu Zhang,Gao-Yuan Zhang,Bon-Chul Koo,Yan Gong,Ruo-Yu Liu,Hidetoshi Sano,Tian-Yu Tu,Yang Chen,Tram N. Le,Lianghao Lin,Friedrich Wyrowski*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用ALMA和APEX望远镜观测超新星遗迹W49B与分子云相互作用区域的HCO+和CO谱线，发现宽线发射区，推断存在强激波，并通过非LTE分析得到高密度和异常高的HCO+/CO丰度比，表明需要极高的宇宙射线电离率（比银河系标准高3–4个数量级），进而探讨低能宇宙射线在激波后介质中的传播及能量贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超新星遗迹与分子云相互作用是理解超新星反馈如何影响星际介质的关键；W49B作为典型中年混合型SNR，其与分子云的相互作用尚未被高分辨率多谱线充分刻画，尤其在化学与激波物理方面缺乏定量约束。

Method: 使用ALMA（HCO+ J=1-0, 3-2, 4-3；CO J=3-2）和APEX（HCO+ J=3-2, 4-3）开展高空间分辨率（~5″）谱线观测；识别宽线（FWHM 25–60 km/s）发射区；结合2.12 μm H₂、1.64 μm [Fe II] 和射电连续谱图像进行空间关联分析；采用非LTE辐射转移模型反演气体密度与HCO+/CO丰度比；结合激波化学模型和宇宙射线电离率（CRIR）参数化，解释高丰度比成因；开展能量预算分析评估激波动能、宇宙射线加热与X射线加热的相对贡献。

Result: 在W49B西部识别出12个HCO+与CO均呈现宽线轮廓的致密区域，空间上与H₂发射一致但偏离[Fe II]和射电峰值；测得激波气体密度为(0.5–1.6)×10⁵ cm⁻³，HCO+/CO丰度比达(4.2–14.5)×10⁻⁴；该丰度比要求CRIR为几×10⁻¹⁴–10⁻¹³ s⁻¹（比标准值高3–4个量级）；部分区域HCO+/CO强度比在高速翼增强，暗示多速度/多密度未分辨激波或CRIR空间变化；另一些区域该比值恒定，支持低能宇宙射线穿透激波后层；能量分析显示激波动能远超CR与X射线加热总和，且CR加热主导X射线加热。

Conclusion: W49B西部存在活跃的SNR-MC激波相互作用，驱动高密度、高电离环境；异常高的HCO+/CO丰度比是强宇宙射线辐照的可靠示踪；低能宇宙射线可在激波后介质中有效传播并主导气体加热，凸显其在超新星反馈能量分配中的关键作用；该结果为理解中年SNR如何通过宇宙射线调控分子云演化提供了直接观测证据。

Abstract: Galactic supernova remnants (SNRs) interacting with molecular clouds (MCs) provide important laboratories for investigating how supernova feedback is transferred to the interstellar medium. We performed observations of the HCO+ J=1-0, 3-2, and 4-3 lines, as well as the CO J=3-2 line, toward the eastern and western regions of SNR W49B using the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) and the Atacama Pathfinder Experiment (APEX) telescope. In W49B west, we identify twelve regions with broad-line profiles in both HCO+ and CO emission, with FWHM of 25-60 km/s, providing clear evidence of SNR-MC interaction. These emissions are compact (at $\sim$5 arcsec resolution) and spatially coincident with 2.12~$μ$m H$_2$ emission, but generally offset from the 1.64~$μ$m [Fe~II] and radio continuum peaks. Non-LTE analyses yield shocked-gas densities of (0.5-1.6) $\times 10^5$~cm$^{-3}$ and high $N_{\rm HCO+}/N_{\rm CO}$ abundance ratios of (4.2-14.5) $\times 10^{-4}$. Reproducing such high abundance ratios in shocked MCs requires high cosmic ray ionization rates (CRIRs) of a few 10$^{-14}$-10$^{-13}$~s$^{-1}$, about 3-4 orders of magnitude above the standard Galactic level. Some regions exhibit enhanced HCO+/CO intensity ratios in their high-velocity line wings, possibly due to multiple unresolved shocks spanning a range of velocities and gas densities, spatial variations in the CRIR, or both. By contrast, the ratio remains nearly constant with velocity in other regions. For these regions, we further explore the propagation of low-energy CRs through the post-shock layer. An energy-budget analysis of a representative shocked clump indicates that the shock-associated kinetic-energy reservoir substantially exceeds cumulative CR and X-ray heating inputs, with CR heating exceeding X-ray heating.[abridged]

</details>


### [113] [On the Existence of an Equilibrium Density Profile for Massive Collisional Star Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13825)
*Ambreesh Khurana,Renyue Cen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文使用CMC-COSMIC蒙特卡洛星团演化代码，研究了致密星团在多种物理过程共同作用下的密度轮廓演化，发现不同初始密度分布的星团最终会收敛到一个特征性的、动力学稳定的King型平衡轮廓（W_0=6.8），且其位力半径扩大约7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解致密星团在多物理过程（如二体弛豫、强相互作用、恒星演化、银河系潮汐）耦合作用下的整体结构演化规律。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛星团演化代码CMC-COSMIC，模拟具有相同初始质量和结合能但不同初始密度分布的致密星团的长期演化，并分析其密度轮廓的收敛性与稳定性。

Result: 星团在晚期准静态演化阶段收敛至一个特征密度轮廓：近似为W_0=6.8的King模型，且最终位力半径约为初始值的7倍；该轮廓是动力学稳定的，偏离后可自发恢复。

Conclusion: 致密星团的长期结构演化由内部能量产生（尤其是黑洞双星）与引力收缩的平衡主导，导致普适的、稳定的平衡密度轮廓，与初始条件无关。

Abstract: The dynamical evolution of massive, dense star clusters is governed by a complex interplay of multiple physical processes, including two-body relaxation, strong encounters, stellar evolution, and galactic tides, superimposed on the orbital motion of individual stars and binaries within the cluster potential. These processes can alter the internal evolution of individual stars and binaries, produce exotic stellar objects, and collectively determine the global structural evolution of the cluster. In this paper, we use the Monte Carlo star cluster evolution code CMC-COSMIC to study how the density profiles of dense star clusters evolve under the combined effects of these processes. We find that for a given initial mass and binding energy, cluster models converge toward a characteristic density profile largely independent of their initial density distributions. This convergence occurs during the late-time, quasi-static phase of evolution, when gravitational contraction is balanced by energy generated through black hole binaries in the cluster core. The shape of this profile is well represented by a King profile with W_0=6.8 but with the virial radius of the final cluster larger than the initial by a factor of about 7. We further show that this equilibrium profile is dynamically stable, i.e., perturbations away from it decay through the cluster's natural dynamical evolution, driving the system back toward the equilibrium profile.

</details>


### [114] [Tracing early galaxies back in time: implications for the dearth of galaxies beyond z~15](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13835)
*L. Ciesla,C. Carvajal-Bohorquez,D. Elbaz,G. Aufort,O. Ilbert,S. Finkelstein,A. M. Koekemoer,P. Oesch,M. Boquien,C. Gruppioni,N. Laporte*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的新型恒星形成历史（SFH）模型，用于推断高红移（z=9–15）星系的早期恒星形成活动；应用该模型分析UNICORN/GOODS-South样本，发现多数星系在z∼12后才形成20%恒星质量，z>15时仅有极少数已显著成星，暗示z∼15–17间恒星形成缓慢衰减，之后加速下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜虽观测到z∼14的星系，但最早期恒星形成仍不可直接探测，需借助观测星系的恒星形成历史（SFH）反推更早时期。现有SFH模型难以准确刻画高红移星系典型的爆发式恒星形成，亟需更物理合理、可验证的新模型。

Method: 构建基于Ornstein-Uhlenbeck随机过程的SFH模型，该模型能自然产生bursty恒星形成行为；在SPHINX²⁰流体动力学模拟星表上进行验证；再应用于UNICORN/GOODS-South中400个z=9–15真实星系，通过贝叶斯拟合推断各星系的累积SFH、形成红移（如z_form,20%）及恒星质量增长时标。

Result: 模型在模拟数据中能准确恢复恒星质量和SFR；对z=10–15的218个星系，仅28个在z=15时已形成20%恒星质量（中位z_form,20%=16.9），24%已达10⁷M⊙；z=17时仅11个满足该条件；z=20时仅1个满足，对应UV星等−18.1±0.6，面密度约1/200 arcmin²；z∼15–17间SFH衰减较缓，之后加速下降。

Conclusion: 高红移星系（z>10）普遍较晚启动显著恒星形成（多晚于z∼12），与z>13已证认星系缺乏清晰演化关联；极早期（z>15）恒星形成稀疏且延迟，支持宇宙再电离主要由z∼10–15星系主导，而非z>17极端早期星系；所提OU-SFH模型为未来JWST高红移星系考古提供可靠工具。

Abstract: Although the James Webb Space Telescope has pushed the observational frontier to $z\sim14$, the onset of star formation in the earliest galaxies remains beyond direct reach, making the star formation histories (SFH) of observed galaxies a key tool for probing earlier epochs. We develop and validate a new SFH model based on an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, capable of reproducing the bursty star formation expected in high-redshift galaxies. Tested on the SPHINX$^{20}$ mock catalogs, it accurately recovers stellar masses and SFRs and reconstructs cumulative SFHs at population level; the formation redshift is well constrained at $z\gtrsim9$ but not at $z\sim8$. Applied to 400 galaxies at $9<z<15$ from the UNICORN/GOODS-South sample, we find that sources observed at $z<10$ typically reached 20% of their stellar mass no earlier than $z\sim12$, disfavoring a clear evolutionary link to spectroscopically confirmed galaxies at $z\gtrsim13$. Among 218 galaxies at $10<z<15$, only 28 had formed 20% of their stellar mass by $z=15$, with a median formation redshift $z_{\rm form,20\%}=16.9^{+1.1}_{-1.1}$, and 24% of our sample had already reached $10^7$M$_{\odot}$ by $z=15$. At $z=17$, only 11 of these 28 galaxies had reached 20% of their stellar mass. The derived survey areas required to detect them are consistent with or slightly more optimistic than previous constraints at the bright end, and larger than some literature estimates at the faint end. At $z=20$, a single galaxy had formed 20% of its stellar mass, yielding a tentative constraint of one object at $M_{\rm UV}=-18.1\pm0.6$ per $\sim200$\,arcmin$^2$. Our results suggest a relatively slow decline in star formation activity between $z\sim15$ and $z\sim17$, followed by a more rapid decline up to $z\sim20$.

</details>


### [115] [Deriving physical parameters of unresolved star clusters. X. Size evolution of young star clusters in the M31 disc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13836)
*Karolis Daugevičius,Eimantas Kriščiūnas,Rima Stonkutė,Vladas Vansevičius*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文分析了M31星系盘中1918个年轻星团（年龄≤8亿年）的结构参数，发现其在最初20 Myr内迅速膨胀，之后呈微弱的年龄-半径关系；簇尺寸与银心距离无显著相关性，表明内部演化主导；质量-半径关系在>100 Myr时呈浅关系，支持恒星演化和动力学相互作用驱动早期结构演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星团在旋涡星系盘中的形成与动力学演化，尤其是结构参数（如尺度）如何随年龄、质量及空间位置变化，为星团演化理论提供观测约束。

Method: 基于Panchromatic Hubble Andromeda Treasury（PHAT）巡天的测光数据构建M31盘星团参数星表，开展统计分析，探究结构参数（如R50）与年龄、质量、银心距离等物理和空间参数的相关性。

Result: M31盘年轻星团初始紧凑（R50≈1.8 pc），前~20 Myr快速膨胀（尺寸翻倍以上）；之后呈弱年龄-半径关系（R50 ∝ t^0.12±0.01）；无显著R50–银心距离相关性；>100 Myr星团呈现浅质量-半径关系（R50 ∝ M^0.18±0.01）；早期演化主要由恒星演化导致的质量损失和内部动力学相互作用驱动。

Conclusion: M31盘星团早期结构演化由内部过程主导，外部潮汐场影响甚微；观测到的多样的质量-半径关系提示当前理论模型仍需完善，尤其在不同星系环境下的普适性方面。

Abstract: Context. Star cluster structural properties and their correlations with other cluster parameters (age, mass, galactocentric distance) provide key constraints on cluster formation and dynamical evolution within star-forming galactic discs. In the tenth paper of this series, we analysed structural parameters of 1918 young star clusters (up to 800 Myr) in the disc of M31. Aims. We aim to examine the structural evolution of M31 disc clusters and determine how their sizes correlate with physical and spatial properties. Our goal is to improve the understanding of cluster formation and evolution in spiral galaxy discs. Methods. We use the M31 disc cluster parameter catalogue based on photometric measurements of the Panchromatic Hubble Andromeda Treasury survey data. We perform statistical analysis to determine how cluster structural parameters relate to their physical characteristics and location within the disc. Results. Young clusters in the disc of M31 are compact ($R_{50}\sim1.8$ pc) and expand rapidly during the first $\sim$20 Myr, more than doubling in size. At older ages, M31 clusters follow a weak age-radius relation $(R_{50}\propto t^{0.12\pm0.01}\,\,)$. We find no significant correlation between cluster size and galactocentric distance, indicating that internal processes dominate their early evolution, with minimal impact from the galactic tidal field. Our results suggest that stellar-evolution-driven mass loss and internal dynamical interactions are likely drivers of early cluster structural evolution in the M31 disc. A shallow mass-radius relation emerges for clusters older than 100 Myr $(R_{50}\propto M^{0.18\pm0.01}\,\,)$, while the trend for younger clusters remains uncertain. The diversity of mass-radius relations observed in nearby spiral galaxies, both within and beyond the Local Group, highlights the need for further theoretical work on cluster formation and evolution.

</details>


### [116] [Measuring the Milky Way potential with the phase snail shape curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13837)
*Rui Guo,Zhao-Yu Li,Juntai Shen,Jie Wang,Shude Mao*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用银河系盘中相位螺旋（phase spirals）的形状来测量银河系垂直势的新方法，并通过模拟和观测数据验证了其有效性，最终给出了银河系晕质量、太阳邻域暗物质密度及扰动时间等关键参数的精确估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位螺旋的存在表明银河盘处于非平衡态，其缠绕形状主要受垂直引力势影响，因此可从中提取势的信息。但其起源尚不清楚，亟需一种可靠方法从观测中反演银河势。

Method: 从密度对比图中提取相位蜗牛（phase snail）形状曲线，通过轨道积分将其变换到垂直频率-作用角（Ω_z-θ_z）空间，再进行线性拟合以推导势参数；并用测试粒子模拟验证该方法。

Result: 方法在模拟中表现优异，恢复的垂直势与输入模型高度一致；应用于观测相位蜗牛后，得到银河系200半径内质量M₂₀₀=0.81⁺⁰·²²₋₀·₂₃×10¹² M⊙，太阳邻域暗物质密度ρ_dm,⊙=0.0076⁺⁰·⁰⁰¹⁹₋₀·₀₀₂₁ M⊙ pc⁻³；联合旋转曲线后，ρ_dm,⊙精度显著提升至0.0097⁺⁰·⁰⁰⁰²₋₀·₀⁰⁰⁹ M⊙ pc⁻³，并估计扰动时间为400–750 Myr（中值564 Myr）。

Conclusion: 相位蜗牛形状是探测银河系垂直势的有效探针；结合旋转曲线可显著提升局部暗物质密度等参数的约束精度，为理解银河系结构与演化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Aims. Phase spirals found in the vertical phase-space ($z-V_z$) indicate that the Galactic disk is in disequilibrium. Although the origin remains elusive, the wrapping of phase snails is dominated by the vertical potential profiles, naturally encoding potential information in their shapes.
  Methods. To measure the Galactic potential, we extracted the phase snail shape curves from density contrast maps and transformed them into the vertical frequency-action angle ($Ω_z - θ_z$) space via orbital integration, then performed linear fitting to derive potential parameters. We verified our method with a test particle simulation.
  Results. Our method performs well in the test particle simulation, with the recovered Galactic vertical potential profiles in excellent agreement with the input potential models. We also apply our method to the observed phase snails with guiding center radii in the range of $8\le R_g\le11$ kpc. Utilizing snail shape curves only, the model derived vertical potential profiles agree with popular Milky Way potential models. The model yields a Galactic virial mass of $M_{200}= 0.81^{+0.22}_{-0.23} \times10^{12}\, {\rm M_{\odot}}$ and a local dark matter density of $ρ_{dm,\odot}= 0.0076^{+0.0019}_{-0.0021} \,{\rm M_{\odot}\,pc^{-3}}$, consistent with the literature, but with a flatter rotation curve than the observation. To better constrain the Galactic potential, we further combine the phase snails and the rotation curve. The improved model results in $M_{200}= 0.87^{+0.31}_{-0.33} \times10^{12}\, {\rm M_{\odot}}$ and $ρ_{dm,\odot}= 0.0097^{+0.0002}_{-0.0009} \,{\rm M_{\odot}\,pc^{-3}}$. The high precision of $ρ_{dm,\odot}$ results from the joint constraints in the solar neighborhood. We also obtain an estimation of the perturbation time in a range of 400-750 Myr with a median of 564 Myr, consistent with recent measurements. (abridged)

</details>


### [117] [Dust in Stellar Populations: Grid-Based and Bayesian Spectral Energy Distribution Fitting of AGB Stars with SEDust and MCM-SEDust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13881)
*Hamidreza Mahani,Atefeh Javadi,Amirhossein Rabiee*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文综述了恒星种群合成（SPS）模型的关键成分，并介绍了SEDust和MCM-SEDust两个自动化管道，用于拟合富尘渐近巨星支（AGB）恒星的光谱能量分布（SED），从而连接单个富尘恒星物理与恒星种群的尘埃预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尘埃在从恒星包层到星系星际介质的各种尺度上显著影响恒星种群的光谱能量分布（SED），需建立能连接单颗富尘恒星物理与整体尘埃预算的模型。

Method: 综述SPS模型关键成分（初始质量函数、恒星演化轨迹、恒星光谱库、尘埃模型），并开发SEDust（基于DUSTY辐射转移模型）及其扩展MCM-SEDust自动化拟合流程。

Result: 提出了SEDust和MCM-SEDust两个工具，可自动拟合富尘AGB恒星SED，并桥接个体恒星尘埃物理与恒星种群尘埃预算。

Conclusion: SEDust系列工具为理解尘埃在恒星种群演化中的作用提供了有效手段，强化了从单星到星系尺度的尘埃建模一致性。

Abstract: Dust shapes the spectral energy distributions (SEDs) of stellar populations on every scale, from the circumstellar envelopes of evolved stars to the interstellar medium of galaxies. We review the ingredients of stellar population synthesis (SPS) models: the initial mass function, stellar evolutionary tracks, stellar spectral libraries, and dust models. We then present SEDust, an automated pipeline that finds the best- fitting DUSTY radiative-transfer model for the SEDs of dusty asymptotic giant branch (AGB) stars, and MCM-SEDust, its extension. We discuss how both connect the physics of individual dusty stars to the dust budget of stellar populations.

</details>


### [118] [A Virgo Environmental Survey Tracing Ionised Gas Emission (VESTIGE). XXIII. NGC 4569 past orbit reconstruction with ORFEO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13951)
*E. Sarpa,L. Pizzuti,A. Boselli,M. Fossati,M. Sun,E. S. Mangola,B. Epinat,G. Hensler,G. De Lucia,L. Cortese,S. Martocchia,M. Fumagalli,P. Côté,J. -C. Cuillandre,L. Ferrarese,S. Gwyn,M. Boquien,F. Renaud,M. A. Taylor,A. Loni,P. Jachym,P. Amram*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文首次将ORFEO模型应用于NGC 4569星系，结合Hα发射区位置与视向速度、引力势与ICM密度分布，反演其轨道与剥离历史；结果支持低距离（16.5 Mpc）、后峰值剥离场景，推得气体尾年龄约250 Myr，峰值压强发生在约163 Myr前，计算高效（仅需约10–11分钟）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延伸电离气体尾可记录星系与星系团内介质（ICM）近期相互作用的动力学信息，但需通过逆动力学问题从观测尾结构中恢复该历史，依赖于气体剥离的物理条件。

Method: 应用ORFEO模型，联合NGC 4569及其94个Hα发射区的天球位置与视向速度、基于MG-MAMPOSSt构建的Virgo A球对称引力势、X射线导出的ICM密度剖面及气体面密度约束，反演轨道与ram-pressure历史；扫描视线距离以检验解的唯一性，并与盘截断半径处独立剥离阈值比较。

Result: 在基准距离d₀=16.5 Mpc下，获得一致相空间序列，尾部动力学年龄T_tail = 250^{+37}_{-19} Myr，ram-pressure峰值时刻t_Pram,peak = -163^{+11}_{-15} Myr，对应近心距305^{+19}_{-25} kpc，表明NGC 4569处于峰值压强之后；距离扫描显示存在低距离（后峰值）与高距离（前峰值）两类拟合解，而盘截断半径的剥离阈值支持低距离解；该解与Hα形态推断的西→东投影运动一致。

Conclusion: ORFEO为剥离星系提供了一种计算高效（单例约10–11分钟）、可扩展的逆建模框架，能可靠导出轨道、尾部及ram-pressure演化历史，有助于指导定制化数值模拟并推广至更大样本。

Abstract: Extended ionised gas tails can preserve a dynamical record of a galaxy's recent interaction with the intracluster medium (ICM). Recovering that history from the observed tail requires solving an inverse dynamical problem tied to the physical conditions for gas removal. We present the first application of ORFEO to NGC 4569, using the sky positions and line-of-sight (LOS) velocities of the galaxy and 94 H$α$-emitting regions to reconstruct its recent orbit and tail chronology. The model combines data with gas surface-density constraints, a spherical Virgo Cluster A gravitational potential derived with MG-MAMPOSSt, and an X-ray-based ICM density profile. For an assumed LOS distance, it infers its orbit and ram-pressure history. At the fiducial distance $d_0=16.5$ Mpc, ORFEO recovers a coherent phase-space sequence with a dynamical tail age $T_{\rm tail}=250^{+37}_{-19}$ Myr. The ram-pressure maximum occurred at $t_{P_{\rm ram},{\rm peak}}=-163^{+11}_{-15}$ Myr, close to the nearest approach to the Virgo Cluster A centre at $305^{+19}_{-25}$ kpc, placing NGC 4569 after peak ram pressure. Scanning the LOS distance does not select a unique solution but separates comparably fitting reconstructions into a low-distance post-peak branch and a high-distance pre-peak branch. Comparison with an independent stripping threshold at the observed disc truncation radius favours the low-distance solutions and supports a post-stripping interpretation. On this branch, ORFEO recovers the mostly west-to-east projected motion inferred from the H$α$ morphology and is consistent with previous constraints on the interaction chronology. Requiring only $\sim10$--11 min of wall-clock time for NGC 4569, ORFEO provides a computationally inexpensive inverse framework for deriving orbital, tail, and ram-pressure histories that can guide tailored simulations and be extended to larger samples of stripped galaxies.

</details>


### [119] [The PAU Survey: Dissecting strong-lensing systems with narrow-band photometric redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14229)
*Shuaibo Geng,Pablo Renard,Qiuhan He,David Navarro-Gironés,Magherita Grespan,Agnieszka Pollo,Enrique Gaztañaga,Marek Biesiada,Ricard Casas,Juan García-Bellido,Pau Tallada-Crespí,Jieci Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于透镜模型引导的测光方法，结合窄带与宽带数据，有效分离强引力透镜系统中前景透镜体与背景源的光，从而更准确地估计二者红移；在8个已知强透镜样本上验证表明，该方法显著提升红移估计精度，尤其对源星系效果明显，并支持红移作为强透镜候选体甄选的独立判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽视场巡天（如Euclid）将发现大量强引力透镜候选体，但其科学应用依赖于透镜体和被透镜源的红移测量；而源星系通常较暗且与前景光混合，红移测定困难。

Method: 采用透镜模型引导的测光：利用宽带模型定义成分区域，通过点反射减法降低前景污染，并融合PAU窄带与HSC/CFHT宽带数据；使用BCNz代码估计透镜与源各自的红移分布，不预设红移大小顺序。

Result: 在8个已知强透镜样本中，所有透镜红移峰值与光谱红移偏差|δ_z|≤0.014；4个低红移源（z_s^spec ≤ 1.23）的红移峰值偏差|δ_z|≤0.025；对称减法使源红移中位偏差从0.336降至0.071；在|δ_z|<0.05精度下，窄+宽带组合获得8个透镜和4个源匹配，优于仅用EAZY+宽带的3个和2个。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效实现混合光系统中透镜与源成分的红移分离，证实红移可作为强透镜候选体评估的可靠补充依据（与形态、颜色并列）；未来更大规模窄带巡天（如J-PAS）有望推广此方法，但仍需不确定性校准和盲测验证。

Abstract: Wide-field imaging surveys such as Euclid will identify large samples of strong-lens candidates, whose scientific use requires redshifts for both deflectors and lensed sources. Source redshifts are challenging because faint arcs are blended with foreground light. We test lens-model-informed photometry on eight known strong lenses, using broad-band models to define component regions, point-reflection subtraction to reduce contamination, and PAU Survey narrow bands combined with five HSC or CFHT broad bands. BCNz estimates separate lens and source redshift distributions without imposing their expected ordering. All eight lens redshift peaks agree with spectroscopy within $|δ_z|\leq0.014$, where $δ_z=(z_b-z_{\rm spec})/(1+z_{\rm spec})$. Four source peaks, all at $z_{\rm s}^{\rm spec}\leq1.23$, agree within $|δ_z|\leq0.025$. Symmetric subtraction reduces the median source $|δ_z|$ from 0.336 to 0.071. At $|δ_z|<0.05$, narrow- plus broad-band photometry yields eight lens and four source matches, versus three and two using EAZY with broad bands alone. These results demonstrate component redshift recovery in blended systems and support redshift-based lens-candidate assessment alongside morphology and colour. Larger narrow-band surveys, including J-PAS, could extend this approach, pending uncertainty calibration and blind candidate tests.

</details>


### [120] [Shot noise, aperture and resolution in Fourier measurements of disc lopsidedness: TNG50-1 and TNG100-1 analysed with TNGGalaxyLab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14278)
*Abbos Omonov,Karamat Mirtadjieva,Salakhutdin Nuritdinov,Dario Dottorini*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TNGGalaxyLab——一个用于模拟盘星系恒星面密度粒子基傅里叶分解的开源Python流程，重点解决因粒子数不足导致的泊松噪声偏差问题，并提出噪声校正方法A1,corr；应用该方法分析IllustrisTNG模拟数据，发现TNG100-1比TNG50-1在相同质量下表现出更高（1.5–1.9倍）且无法由动力学参数解释的偏心度，挑战了先前关于TNG100分辨率不足导致低偏心度的解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决粒子模拟中因有限粒子数引起的傅里叶振幅（尤其是m=1偏心度A1）的泊松噪声上偏问题，实现跨分辨率模拟（如TNG50与TNG100）间偏心度的可靠比较。

Method: 开发TNGGalaxyLab开源Python流程，实现Rix & Zaritsky (1995)归一化下的m=1–4傅里叶振幅与相位剖面测量；引入随机化方位角法定义每星系噪声基底，并提出噪声校正振幅A1,corr；在统一孔径（[1.5,2.5] Rd）下处理TNG50-1和TNG100-1中央星系样本，并进行降采样与偏差校正实验。

Result: A1,corr在校正后使TNG100/TNG50中位A1比从2.3降至1.6；在固定恒星质量下，TNG100-1盘星系仍比TNG50-1偏心度高1.5–1.9倍，该差异在3.5–6 kpc最显著，且不能由kappa_rot或D/T解释；TNG50-1与观测S4G样本一致，而TNG100-1不一致；Lokas (2022)报道的TNG100低A1并非分辨率缺陷所致。

Conclusion: 傅里叶偏心度测量必须进行粒子噪声校正；未校正时跨分辨率比较会产生误导性结论；TNG100-1系统性高估偏心度，提示其结构形成物理或数值实现可能存在尚未识别的偏差。

Abstract: We present TNGGalaxyLab, an open-source Python pipeline for particle-based Fourier decomposition of the stellar surface density of simulated disc galaxies. It measures m = 1-4 amplitude and phase profiles in the Rix and Zaritsky (1995) normalisation, handles IllustrisTNG snapshot conventions, and recovers injected amplitudes in synthetic discs to better than 2 per cent at N = 5 x 10^5. Fourier amplitudes are positive-definite, so Poisson noise biases them upwards by an amount that grows as the particle number falls. We measure a per-galaxy noise floor by randomising particle azimuths and define a noise-corrected amplitude A1,corr. Downsampling TNG50-1 discs to the TNG100-1 particle mass inflates the uncorrected A1 by 40-60 per cent, while A1,corr is recovered to within approximately 10 per cent. We then process all central galaxies with 3.16 x 10^9 < M*/Msun < 10^11 in TNG50-1 (915) and TNG100-1 (6717) with identical settings in the [1.5, 2.5] Rd aperture. Removing the bootstrap and shot-noise biases lowers the TNG100/TNG50 ratio of median A1 from 2.3 to 1.6. At fixed stellar mass, kinematically selected TNG100-1 discs remain 1.5-1.9 times more lopsided after correction; this excess persists whether the aperture is defined in Rd or in kpc, is not explained by kappa_rot or D/T, and is largest at 3.5-6 kpc. TNG50-1 is statistically consistent with the mass-matched S4G sample of Zaritsky et al. (2013); TNG100-1 is not. The low A1 reported for TNG100 by Lokas (2022) cannot be read as a resolution deficit, since with matched measurements TNG100-1 is more lopsided than TNG50-1. Code and catalogues are public.

</details>


### [121] [Tracing the Epoch of Reionization through extreme emission line galaxies around Cosmic Noon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14332)
*M. Delgado Mancheño,J. Gallego,S. Pascual,M. Tang,R. Pello,N. Laporte,A. Camps-Fariña,M. Chamorro-Cazorla*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过CANDELS/EGS数据构建并表征了宇宙正午（z~1.0–1.7）的极端发射线星系（EELGs）大样本，验证其为z>5再电离主导星系的可靠低红移类似体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: z>6恒星形成星系被认为是宇宙再电离的主要驱动者，但其极暗弱难以详细研究；EELGs作为低红移、致密、低质量、强发射的星系，可能是其理想类似体，需系统验证。

Method: 基于CANDELS光度数据，采用J波段色余准则筛选Hβ+[OIII]与Hα发射体；结合MMT/MMIRS、Keck/MOSFIRE、HST/WFC3及JWST/NIRSpec等多平台光谱观测进行验证，并辅以GTC/OSIRIS+新数据；采用贝叶斯联合拟合并做完备性校正。

Result: 获得102个z~1.7的Hβ+[OIII]发射体和127个z~1.0的Hα发射体；均致密（~1.6 kpc）、低质量（log(M⋆/M⊙)~8）、高比恒星形成率（sSFR~10⁻⁸ yr⁻¹）；光谱确认纯度高（85%与63%），无显著AGN污染；质量-红移关系为log(M⋆/M⊙) = (6.71±0.16) + (3.31±0.46)log(1+z)。

Conclusion: 构建了高纯度、无AGN污染的宇宙正午EELG样本；其物理性质与z>5 JWST探测到的极端发射星系一致，证实EELGs是再电离时期星系的可靠中红移类似体。

Abstract: Star-forming galaxies at z>6 are thought to be the main drivers of cosmic reionization, but their faintness makes detailed study challenging even with JWST. Extreme Emission Line Galaxies (EELGs) are compact, low-mass systems with intense nebular emission and are potential lower-redshift analogues of this population. We aim to assemble and characterize a statistically significant sample of EELGs in the CANDELS/EGS field around cosmic noon, and to assess their validity as analogues of the galaxies that drove reionization. We select EELG candidates from CANDELS photometry with a J-band color-excess criterion that identifies both H$β$+[OIII] and H$α$ emitters. Physical properties are taken from CANDELS data products, and the selection is validated with MMT/MMIRS, Keck/MOSFIRE, HST/WFC3 and JWST/NIRSpec spectroscopy, complemented by our own GTC/OSIRIS+ program. We identify 102 H$β$+[OIII] emitters at $z\sim1.7$ and 127 H$α$ emitters at $z\sim1.0$. They are compact ($\sim$1.6 kpc), low-mass ($\log(M_\star/M_\odot)\sim8$) and strongly star-forming (sSFR $\sim10^{-8}$ yr$^{-1}$). Estimated purities of $84.3\pm1.5$\% (H$β$+[OIII]) and $76.8\pm2.0$\% (H$α$) agree with spectroscopic confirmation rates (85\% and 63\%), with no evidence of significant AGN contamination. A joint Bayesian fit, corrected for completeness via a truncated likelihood, yields $\log(M_\star/M_\odot) = (6.71\pm0.16) + (3.31\pm0.46)\log(1+z)$, suggesting an evolution in the characteristic mass of the sample with cosmic time. We spectroscopically confirm a highly pure, AGN-free sample of cosmic-noon EELGs. While their mass scale evolves with redshift, their properties consistently match those of z>5 JWST extreme emitters, supporting them as intermediate-redshift analogues.

</details>


### [122] [A Statistical Analysis of Lopsidedness in Disk Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14435)
*Abbos Omonov,Karomat Mirtadjieva,Salakhutdin Nuritdinov,Dario Dottorini*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文对606个盘星系的傅里叶不对称性（m=1和m=2模）进行了统计分析，发现A1和A2均服从对数正态分布，彼此正相关，且A1随形态类型T增加而增大、随恒星质量与旋转速度增大而减小，表明内部动力学主导llopsidedness；TNG50-1模拟也再现了A1–恒星质量反相关趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解盘星系中m=1（lopsidedness）和m=2（椭率/棒）傅里叶模式的统计性质及其物理起源，区分内部动力学与外部潮汐扰动的相对作用。

Method: 基于去重的606个星系样本（442个含A1，302个同时含A1和A2），进行K-S检验、Spearman/Kendall相关性分析及分布拟合；并与TNG50-1宇宙学模拟结果对比。

Result: （i）A1与A2均显著服从对数正态分布；（ii）A2与A1线性正相关（rho=0.501）；（iii）A1随T型单调上升；（iv）A1与气体旋转速度和恒星质量显著负相关；（v）TNG50-1模拟复现A1–恒星质量反相关，但整体A1偏低约1.4倍。

Conclusion: 盘星系的lopsidedness主要受内部动力学调控，而非仅由外部潮汐扰动驱动；其幅度分布特征支持乘性放大机制；数值模拟在定性趋势上与观测一致，验证了当前星系形成模型的相关物理刻画。

Abstract: We present a statistical analysis of Fourier lopsidedness in disk galaxies based on a deduplicated compilation of 606 unique objects: 442 have a measured m = 1 amplitude (A1), and 302 of these also have a measured m = 2 amplitude (A2), with no entry carrying A2 alone. The sample is a factor of approximately 3 larger than earlier homogeneous A1-A2 datasets. Our main results are: (i) Both A1 and A2 follow log-normal distributions (KS D = 0.046 and 0.048) which are strongly preferred over Rayleigh or exponential forms, pointing to a multiplicative amplification process rather than random-phase superposition. (ii) The m = 1 and m = 2 modes are correlated as A2 = 0.29 A1 + 0.11 (Spearman rho = 0.501, p less than 10^-19). (iii) A1 rises monotonically with morphological T-type from approximately 0.07 in E/S0 to approximately 0.37 in Sm/Im galaxies (Kendall tau = 0.234, p approximately 10^-12). (iv) A1 anti-correlates with gas rotation velocity (Spearman rho = -0.432) and, in a stellar-mass cross-matched subsample of 246 galaxies with independent stellar-mass estimates, with stellar mass (rho = -0.395). The weaker lopsidedness of massive, fast-rotating disks indicates that the observed m = 1 amplitude is regulated by internal dynamics rather than set by tidal perturbations alone. (v) The TNG50-1 simulation, analysed through the TNGGalaxyLab Fourier pipeline, reproduces the observed A1-stellar-mass anti-correlation with a nearly identical Spearman coefficient, although its median A1 is approximately 1.4 times lower, an offset consistent with the heterogeneous target selection of the observational compilation.

</details>


### [123] [Dwarf galaxy simulations with stellar-core mass- and metallicity-dependent core-collapse supernova explosion energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14437)
*Natalia Lahén,Kiril Maltsev,Thorsten Naab,Hans-Thomas Janka*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的核心坍缩超新星（CCSN）爆发能力与爆炸能量模型，考虑了恒星质量、金属丰度及黑洞形成机制，并在SPHGal模拟中验证其对星系演化的影响；结果表明，较传统1Berg模型，该模型导致更平稳的恒星形成、更弱的外流、更高的星团形成效率和更强的局域化学增丰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统星系模拟中常采用固定1贝克勒尔（1B=10^51 erg）的超新星反馈能量，忽略了真实CCSN能量随前身星性质变化及失败超新星（即黑洞形成）的物理效应，导致对星系反馈、物质循环和化学演化的刻画存在系统性偏差。

Method: 基于三维CCSN数值模拟校准，构建了依赖于核心质量和金属丰度的恒星可爆发性（explodability）与爆炸能量E_expl（0.1–2 B）模型；将其嵌入SPHGal光滑粒子流体动力学星系演化代码中，开展低金属丰度孤立星系的多组对比模拟，系统分析不同恒星反馈模型对星系演化的影响。

Result: 新模型使黑洞形成率提高近三倍；但因失败CCSN占比低，对恒星形成率影响甚微；关键效应源于平均爆炸能量降至约0.33 B，导致恒星形成更持续、外流减弱、质量加载因子下降、星团形成效率提升、星形成区化学增丰与金属再循环增强。

Conclusion: CCSN反馈能量不应设为固定值，而应由恒星演化与爆炸物理决定；采用更真实的能量分布显著改变星系尺度反馈效应，挑战了当前高分辨率模拟中依赖强超新星反馈匹配观测关系的传统范式。

Abstract: Core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe) are a critical source of thermal and kinetic energy and chemically enriched material in galaxies. Leveraging recent advances in CCSN modelling, we implement a novel core-mass- and metallicity-dependent prescription for stellar explodability and CCSN explosion energies, $E_\mathrm{expl}$, in the SPHGal galaxy evolution code. It accounts for black hole (BH) formation via SN failures and via partial fallback of ejecta mass, and adopts an explosion-progenitor connection calibrated to three-dimensional CCSN simulations, such that $E_\mathrm{expl}$ varies between $<0.1$-$2\, \mathrm{B}$. We ran simulations of isolated low-metallicity galaxies with variations in the input stellar feedback models. With the new explodability prescription nearly three times more BHs form compared to the canonical CCSN-model, but the reduced number of successful CCSNe has minimal impact on galactic star formation owing to the low fraction of failed CCSNe. While BH formation contributes to removal of matter from the galactic baryon cycle, the latter remains driven by galactic outflows, and most compact remnant mass is stored in white dwarves. The most significant change is instead brought about by the new average-$E_\mathrm{expl}$ that amounts to $\sim33$\% of the $1\,\mathrm{B}=10^{51}\, \mathrm{erg}$ traditionally assumed in sub-resolution CCSN-models. This reduction results in increased and less bursty star formation, reduced outflows and mass-loading factors, increased star cluster formation efficiency, and enhanced chemical enrichment and metal-recycling in star-forming regions. These results stand in contrast with outflow-launching and baryon cycle modelling in lower-resolution galaxy simulations in which canonical (i.e., $1\, \mathrm{B}$) CCSN feedback is typically assumed to produce galaxy models that match observed scaling relations.

</details>


### [124] [r-Process Abundance Variations in Globular Clusters as Fossils of Turbulent Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14472)
*Lailani Kenoly,Isabelle Connor,Melinda Soares-Furtado,Evan N. Kirby,Nia Imara,Enrico Ramirez-Ruiz*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过小尺度数值模拟研究了银河系贫金属球状星团中r-过程元素丰度差异的起源，发现其可能源于星团形成前星际介质中r-过程喷出物（如铕）因超新星驱动湍流混合不充分而产生的长期化学不均匀性，并随金属丰度增加而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释贫金属球状星团中观测到的显著r-过程丰度（如[Eu/Fe]）星间差异及其随金属丰度[Fe/H]升高而减弱的现象，探究该结构是否源于星团形成前气体环境的不完全混合。

Method: 采用秒差距（parsec）尺度的数值模拟，追踪超新星驱动湍流盘中r-过程元素（以Eu为代表）的输运与混合过程，分析不同空间尺度下、对应恒星形成区域的气体丰度结构演化。

Result: 模拟显示稀有增丰事件可产生强且持久的[Eu/Fe]空间不均匀性；随着化学演化进行，整体丰度方差减小，大尺度平均削弱差异；低[Fe/H]时存在显著预坍缩r-过程丰度弥散，且其幅度随[Fe/H]升高呈类似观测的下降趋势。

Conclusion: 观测到的部分r-过程丰度变化很可能继承自星团诞生前气体中的原始化学结构，球状星团因而可作为记录早期罕见增丰事件与恒星形成之间混合历史的‘化石’。

Abstract: Several metal-poor Galactic globular clusters exhibit substantial star-to-star $r$-process abundance variations at nearly fixed [Fe/H], with the ensemble suggesting smaller variations toward increasing metallicity. We investigate whether this chemical structure can be inherited from incomplete mixing in the galactic environments in which the clusters formed. Using parsec-scale simulations of rare-element transport in a supernova-driven turbulent disk, we follow the redistribution of $r$-process ejecta and characterize the abundance structure of gas selected to trace star-forming environments. Rare enrichment events generate strong [Eu/Fe] inhomogeneities that persist across a range of physical scales. As chemical evolution proceeds, the available abundance variance decreases, while spatial averaging over larger regions reduces the differences between their characteristic abundances. The simulations provide substantial pre-collapse $r$-process variance at low [Fe/H] and show the same broad decline toward higher metallicity suggested by the globular-cluster measurements. Our results support an interpretation in which at least some of the observed $r$-process variations are inherited from pre-existing chemical structure in the natal environment, allowing globular clusters to preserve a fossil record of the mixing history between rare enrichment and star formation.

</details>
